c^{2}_{s}=\gamma\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-1)\rho}.

(2.16)

Applying the curl-operator to the first equation in (
1.8
), we find that

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v_{k})=&-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}v_{k}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-1)\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}h\\
=&-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}(\partial_{m}v_{k}-\partial_{k}v_{m})-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{k}v_{m}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-1)\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}h\\
=&-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon_{mk}^{\ \ l}w_{l}\partial_{j}v^{m}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-1)\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}h\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{\rho}(\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla)v^{i}-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{i}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-1)\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

As a result, we get

\mathbf{T}w^{i}=(\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla)v^{i}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}h.

Then Equation (
2.4
) is derived. On the other hand, it’s direct for us to calculate

\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}=\mathrm{div}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}^{i})=-(\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla)\rho.

So we have proved (
2.5
). Applying the curl-operator to (
2.4
), we have

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}w_{k}+\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{m}v_{k}\partial_{j}w^{m}+\epsilon^{ijk}w^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{j}v_{k}\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon^{kml}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{m}h)\\
=&-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}w_{k}+\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{m}v_{k}\partial_{j}w^{m}+w^{m}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{i})\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon^{kml}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{m}h)\end{split}

(2.17)

Calculate

\begin{split}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}w_{k}=&-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}(\partial_{m}w_{k}-\partial_{k}w_{m})-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{k}w_{m}\\
=&(\delta^{i}_{m}\delta^{j}_{l}-\delta^{i}_{l}\delta^{j}_{m})\partial_{j}v^{m}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{l}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{k}w_{m}\\
=&\partial_{j}v^{i}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{j}-\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{k}w_{m},\end{split}

(2.18)

and

\begin{split}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{m}v_{k}\partial_{j}w^{m}=&\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}w^{m}(\partial_{m}v_{k}-\partial_{k}v_{m})+\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}w^{m}\partial_{k}v_{m}\\
=&-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{j}\partial_{j}w^{i}+\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{i}\partial_{j}w^{j}+\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}w^{m}\partial_{k}v_{m}\\
=&-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{j}\partial_{j}w^{i}-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{i}w^{j}\partial_{j}\rho+\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}w^{m}\partial_{k}v_{m}\\
=&-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{j}\partial_{j}w^{i}-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{i}w^{j}\partial_{j}\rho-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v_{m}\partial_{k}w^{m}.\end{split}

(2.19)

Inserting (
2.18
) and (
2.19
) to (
2.17
), we get

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&(\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla)v^{i}-\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}-2\epsilon^{imn}\partial_{m}v^{j}\partial_{n}w_{j}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial^{i}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-2}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{l}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\Delta\rho\partial^{i}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial^{i}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial^{m}h-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial^{i}\rho\Delta h.\end{split}

(2.20)

So we have proved (
2.6
). Multiplying with
\mathrm{e^{-\rho}}
, we can update (
2.20
) by

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{i})=&(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla)v^{i}-2\epsilon^{imn}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{m}v^{j}\partial_{n}w_{j}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial^{i}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-2}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{l}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\Delta\rho\partial^{i}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial^{i}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial^{m}h-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial^{i}\rho\Delta h.\end{split}

(2.21)

Applying the operator
\mathrm{curl}
to (
2.21
) yields

\begin{split}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\left(-2\epsilon_{kmn}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{m}v^{a}\partial^{n}w_{a}\right)+\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{k})\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{k}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\partial_{l}\rho+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{k}h\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{l}h-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{l}h\partial_{j}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}h\partial_{j}\partial_{k}\rho-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{l}h\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\Delta\rho\partial_{k}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial^{m}h\partial_{m}\partial_{k}\rho-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\Delta h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}\Delta h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\Delta\rho\\
&+2\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h.\end{split}

(2.22)

Calculate

\begin{split}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{k})=&\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{m})\partial_{m}v_{k}+\epsilon^{ijk}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{m})\partial_{j}\partial_{m}v_{k}\\
=&\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{m})\partial_{m}v_{k}+w^{m}w^{i}\partial_{m}\rho+w^{m}\partial_{m}w^{i}\\
=&\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{m}v_{k}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{m})-\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial_{m}v_{k}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{m}\\
&+\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{2}_{jm}v_{k}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{m}.\end{split}

(2.23)

Substituting (
2.23
) in (
2.22
), we find that

\begin{split}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon_{kmn}\partial_{j}(\partial^{m}v^{a})\partial^{n}w_{a}-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{kmn}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial^{m}v^{a}\partial_{j}(\partial^{n}w_{a})\\
&+\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{m}v_{k}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{m})-\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial_{m}v_{k}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{m}+\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{2}_{jm}v_{k}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{m}\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{k}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\partial_{l}\rho+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{k}h\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{l}h-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{l}h\partial_{j}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}h\partial_{j}\partial_{k}\rho-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{l}h\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\Delta\rho\partial_{k}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial^{m}h\partial_{m}\partial_{k}\rho-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\Delta h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}\Delta h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\Delta\rho\\
&+2\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h+2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{kmn}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{m}v^{a}\partial_{n}w_{a}\partial_{j}{\rho}.\end{split}

For simplicity, we rewrite

\begin{split}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon_{kmn}\partial_{j}(\partial^{m}v^{a})\partial^{n}w_{a}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\Delta\rho\\
&+2\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h+Z^{i}_{1},\end{split}

(2.24)

where

\begin{split}Z^{i}_{1}=&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{k}h-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{kmn}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial^{m}v^{a}\partial_{j}(\partial^{n}w_{a})\\
&+2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{kmn}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{m}v^{a}\partial_{n}w_{a}\partial_{j}{\rho}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\partial_{l}\rho+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{k}h\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{l}h-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{l}h\partial_{j}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{l}h\partial_{j}\partial_{k}\rho-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{l}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{l}h\\
&+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\Delta\rho\partial_{k}h+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial^{m}h\partial_{m}\partial_{k}\rho-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho})\partial_{k}\rho\Delta h\\
&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}\rho\partial_{j}\Delta h\end{split}

(2.25)

can be viewed as a lower order term.
The third term of the right hand of (
2.24
) causes some difficulty. We use
\partial^{m}\rho=-c^{-2}_{s}\mathbf{T}v^{m}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial^{m}h
to calculate

\begin{split}2\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}\rho\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h=&\mathbf{T}(-\frac{2}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)+Z^{i}_{2},\end{split}

(2.26)

where

\begin{split}Z^{i}_{2}=&-\frac{2}{\gamma}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h\\
&+\frac{2}{\gamma}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{j}(c^{-2}_{s})(c^{2}_{s}\partial^{m}\rho-\frac{1}{\gamma}c^{2}_{s}\partial^{m}h)\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h\\
&-\frac{2}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{n}\partial_{n}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h+\frac{4}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\partial_{a}v^{a}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{n}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h\\
&-\frac{2}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}(\partial_{m}v^{a}\partial_{a}\partial_{k}h-\partial_{a}h\partial_{m}\partial_{k}v^{a}-\partial_{k}v^{a}\partial_{a}\partial_{m}h).\end{split}

(2.27)

Similarly, we have

\begin{split}&\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\Delta\rho\\
=&-\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\Delta(-c^{-2}_{s}\mathbf{T}v_{j})-\frac{1}{\gamma}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\Delta h\\
=&\mathbf{T}(\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})+Z^{i}_{3},\end{split}

(2.28)

where

\begin{split}Z^{i}_{3}=&\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{a}v^{a}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}v^{a}\partial_{a}h\Delta v_{j}\\
&+\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}h(\partial^{m}v^{a}\partial_{m}\partial_{a}v_{j}+\Delta v^{a}\partial_{a}v_{j})-\frac{1}{\gamma}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\Delta h\\
&+\frac{1}{\gamma}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\partial^{m}(c^{-2}_{s})\partial_{m}(c^{2}_{s}\partial_{j}\rho-\frac{1}{\gamma}c^{2}_{s}\partial_{j}h)\\
&+\frac{1}{\gamma}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial_{k}h\Delta(-c^{-2}_{s})(c^{2}_{s}\partial_{j}\rho-\frac{1}{\gamma}c^{2}_{s}\partial_{j}h).\end{split}

(2.29)

By using
\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon_{kmn}=\delta^{j}_{m}\delta^{i}_{n}-\delta^{i}_{m}\delta^{j}_{n}
, we can obtain

\begin{split}-2\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon_{kmn}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}(\partial^{m}v^{a})\partial^{n}w_{a}=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}(\delta^{j}_{m}\delta^{i}_{n}-\delta^{i}_{m}\delta^{j}_{n})\partial_{j}(\partial^{m}v^{a})\partial_{n}w_{a}\\
=&\Xi_{1}+\Xi_{2},\end{split}

(2.30)

where

\begin{split}\Xi_{1}:=-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\Delta v^{a}\partial^{i}w_{a},\quad\Xi_{2}:=2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{i}(\partial_{j}v^{a})\partial^{j}w_{a}.\end{split}

(2.31)

The Hodge decomposition yields

\begin{split}\Delta\mathbf{v}=\partial\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}-\mathrm{curl}^{2}\mathbf{v}&=-\partial\mathbf{T}{\rho}+\mathrm{curl}\big(\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathbf{w}\big)\\
&=-\mathbf{T}(\partial{\rho})+\partial v^{k}\partial_{k}{\rho}++\mathrm{curl}\big(\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathbf{w}\big).\end{split}

(2.32)

Substituting (
2.32
) in
\Xi_{1}
, we can rewrite
\Xi_{1}
as

\begin{split}\Xi_{1}=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\big[-\mathbf{T}(\partial^{a}{\rho})+\partial^{a}v^{k}\partial_{k}{\rho}-\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\epsilon^{ajk}\partial_{j}{\rho}w_{k}-\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{a}\big]\partial^{i}w_{a}\\
=&\mathbf{T}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}\big)-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\mathbf{T}(\partial^{i}w_{a})+2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}\\
&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}v^{k}\partial_{k}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{a}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\epsilon^{ajk}\partial_{j}{\rho}w_{k}\partial^{i}w_{a}\\
=&\mathbf{T}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}\big)-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\mathbf{T}(\partial^{i}w_{a})\\
&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}v^{k}\partial_{k}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{a}\partial^{i}w_{a}\\
&+2\epsilon^{ajk}\partial_{j}{\rho}w_{k}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}.\end{split}

(2.33)

In (
2.33
), it remains for us to write
-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\mathbf{T}(\partial^{i}w_{a})
in a suitable way. Note
\mathbf{T}w_{a}=w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{a}+\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\epsilon_{amn}\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{m}h
. Then we can calculate that

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\partial^{i}w_{a})=&\partial^{i}(\mathbf{T}w_{a})-[\partial^{i},\mathbf{T}]w_{a}\\
=&\partial^{i}w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{a}+w^{m}\partial^{i}(\partial_{m}v_{a})-\partial^{i}v^{k}\partial_{k}w_{a}\\
&+\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\partial^{i}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{m}h\\
&+\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial^{i}\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{m}h\\
&+\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{i}\partial^{m}h.\end{split}

We therefore derive that

\begin{split}&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\mathbf{T}(\partial^{i}w_{a})\\
=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\big(\partial^{i}w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{a}+w^{m}\partial^{i}(\partial_{m}v_{a})-\partial^{i}v^{k}\partial_{k}w_{a}\big)\\
&-2\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{m}h\\
&-2\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{m}h)\\
&-2\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial^{a}{\rho}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho}\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{i}\partial^{m}h.\end{split}

(2.34)

Substituting (
2.34
) in (
2.33
), we have

\begin{split}\Xi_{1}=&\mathbf{T}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}\big)+Z_{4}^{i},\end{split}

(2.35)

where

\begin{split}Z^{i}_{4}=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}v^{k}\partial_{k}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}W^{a}\partial^{i}w_{a}\\
&+2\epsilon^{ajk}\partial_{j}{\rho}w_{k}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}\\
&-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\big(\partial^{i}w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{a}+w^{m}\partial^{i}(\partial_{m}v_{a})-\partial^{i}v^{k}\partial_{k}w_{a}\big)\\
&-2\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}(\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-2)\rho})\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{m}h\\
&-2\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{m}h\partial^{i}\partial^{n}\rho\\
&-2\epsilon_{amn}\bar{\rho}^{\gamma-1}\mathrm{e}^{h+(\gamma-3)\rho}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{n}\rho\partial^{i}\partial^{m}h.\end{split}

(2.36)

Finally, let us consider
\Xi_{2}
. By using chain rule, we obtain

\begin{split}\Xi_{2}=\ &2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{i}(\partial_{j}v^{a})\partial^{j}w_{a}\\
=\ &\partial^{i}(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}w_{a})+2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{i}{\rho}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}w_{a}-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}(\partial^{i}w_{a})\\
=\ &\partial^{i}(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}w_{a})+\tilde{Z}^{i}_{5},\end{split}

(2.37)

where

\tilde{Z}^{i}_{5}=2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{i}{\rho}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}w_{a}-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}(\partial^{i}w_{a}).

(2.38)

Inserting (
2.70
)-(
2.38
) to (
2.30
), we can update (
2.30
) by

\begin{split}-2\epsilon^{ijk}\epsilon_{kmn}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}(\partial^{m}v^{a})\partial^{n}w_{a}=\mathbf{T}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}\big)+\partial^{i}(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}w_{a})+K_{4}^{i}+Z^{i}_{5}.\end{split}

(2.39)

Substituting (
2.26
),(
2.28
), and (
2.39
) in (
2.24
), we find

\begin{split}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&\partial^{i}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{b}\big)+Z^{i}_{1}+Z^{i}_{2}+Z^{i}_{3}+Z^{i}_{4}+Z^{i}_{5}\\
&+\mathbf{T}\big(-2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}-\frac{2}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h-\frac{1}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j}\big).\end{split}

(2.40)

By chain rule, we then get

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\mathrm{curl}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})\right\}=\mathbf{T}\left\{\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w_{k})\right\}&=\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w_{k})+\epsilon^{ijk}[\mathbf{T},\partial_{j}](\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w_{k})\\
&=\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w^{i})-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w_{k}).\end{split}

(2.41)

Substituting (
2.40
) in (
2.41
) yields

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\mathrm{curl}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})\right\}=&\mathbf{T}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}-\frac{2}{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{-2\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h+\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j}\big)\\
&+\partial^{i}\big(2\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{n}v^{a}\partial^{n}w_{a}\big)+Z^{i}_{1}+Z^{i}_{2}+Z^{i}_{3}+Z^{i}_{4}+Z^{i}_{5}.\end{split}

(2.42)

Here

\begin{split}Z^{i}_{5}=&\tilde{Z}^{i}_{5}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}w_{k})\\
=&\tilde{Z}^{i}_{5}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k}).\end{split}

(2.43)

Multiplying
\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}
on (
2.42
), we conclude

\begin{split}&\mathbf{T}\left\{\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})-2\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j}\right\}\\
=&\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)+K^{i},\end{split}

(2.44)

where
K^{i}=\sum_{a=1}^{6}K^{i}_{a}
,
K_{a}^{i}=\mathrm{e}^{\rho}Z^{i}_{a},(a=1,2,\cdots,5)
and
K_{6}^{i}
is as stated in (
2.15
). We also note
\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{w}^{i})=\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\rho\cdot\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k}
, so we can obtain (
2.7
) if we use (
2.44
).
∎

Lemma 2.5

(transport equations for
\mathbf{H}
)
.

Let
\mathbf{H}=(H^{1},H^{2},H^{3})^{\mathrm{T}}
be defined in (
1.6
). Then
\mathbf{H}
satisfies

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}H^{i}=\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}v^{j}\partial^{i}h-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h-\epsilon^{ijk}w_{k}\partial_{j}h,\end{split}

(2.45)

Moreover, we have

\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{H}^{i}=-\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}\rho\partial_{k}h,

(2.46)

and

\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})=-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}h,

(2.47)

and

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\partial^{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}=&-2\partial^{k}(\partial_{i}v^{j})\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+Y^{k}.\end{split}

(2.48)

Above, we have denoted

\begin{split}Y^{k}=&-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial^{k}\partial^{i}(\partial_{j}h)+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial^{k}\rho\partial_{j}h+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial^{k}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}h\\
&+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial_{j}h\partial_{k}\partial^{i}\rho+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial^{k}\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

(2.49)

Proof.

Firstly, taking derivatives of
\mathbf{T}h=0
, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{t}(\partial^{i}h)+(\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla)(\partial^{i}h)=&-\partial^{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}h\\
=&-(\partial^{i}v^{j}-\partial^{j}v^{i})\partial_{j}h-\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h\\
=&-\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\epsilon^{ijk}w_{k}\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

(2.50)

By using (
2.50
) and (
1.8
), we derive that

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)=&-\partial^{i}h\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{T}h+\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathbf{T}(\partial^{i}h)\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}v^{j}\partial^{i}h-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h-\epsilon^{ijk}w_{k}\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

(2.51)

Taking derivative
\partial_{i}
of (
2.51
), we get

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\partial_{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)\right\}=&-\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{i}\rho(\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h+\partial_{j}v^{j}\partial^{i}h)-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{i}w_{k}\partial_{j}h-\epsilon^{ijk}w_{k}\partial_{ij}h\\
&+\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\Delta h\partial_{j}v^{j}+\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h\partial_{i}(\partial_{j}v^{j})-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{j}h\partial^{j}(\partial_{i}v^{i})-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{i}(\partial_{j}h)\\
=&-2\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}(\partial^{i}h)+\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\Delta h\cdot\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}-\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{i}\rho(\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h+\partial_{j}v^{j}\partial^{i}h)\\
&-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{i}w_{k}\partial_{j}h-\epsilon^{ijk}w_{k}\partial_{ij}h+\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial_{i}\rho\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

(2.52)

Therefore, we can deduce that

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)\right\}=&\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathbf{T}\left\{\partial_{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)\right\}+\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)\mathbf{T}\rho\\
=&\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathbf{T}\left\{\partial_{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\partial^{i}h)\right\}+\partial_{i}\rho\ \partial^{i}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}-\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\Delta h\\
=&-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}(\partial^{i}h)-\partial_{i}\rho(\partial^{j}v^{i}\partial_{j}h+\partial_{j}v^{j}\partial^{i}h)\\
&-\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}w_{k}\partial_{j}h-\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{k}\partial_{ij}h+\partial_{i}\rho\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}h+\partial_{i}\rho\ \partial^{i}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\\
=&-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h-\partial_{i}\rho\partial_{j}h(\partial_{i}v^{j}-\partial^{j}v^{i})-\epsilon^{ijk}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{j}h\partial_{i}w_{k}\\
=&-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

(2.53)

Taking derivatives
\partial^{k}
of (
2.53
) yields

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\partial^{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}=&\partial^{k}\left\{-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}h\right\}\\
=&-2\partial^{k}(\partial_{i}v^{j})\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+Y^{k},\end{split}

(2.54)

where

\begin{split}Y^{k}=&-2\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial^{k}\rho\partial_{j}h+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial^{k}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}h\\
&+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial_{j}h\partial_{k}\partial^{i}\rho+\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial^{k}\partial_{j}h.\end{split}

(2.55)

∎

Remark 2.1
.

This lemma is different from
[
50
, Lemma 5.3]
, since we take third-order derivatives on the transport equations. We are not able to prove the lemma directly by taking spatial derivatives as in
[
50
, Lemma 5.3]
or
[
16
, Proposition 2.1]
.

2.2.
Commutator estimates and product estimates

We start by recalling some commutator estimates and product estimates, which have been proved in references
[
25
,
47
,
59
,
32
,
42
]
.

Lemma 2.6
.

[
25
]

Let
a\geq 0
. Then for any scalar function
f_{1},f_{2}
, we have

\|\Lambda_{x}^{a}(f_{1}f_{2})-(\Lambda_{x}^{a}f_{1})f_{2}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim\|\Lambda_{x}^{a-1}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial f_{2}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}+\|f_{1}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{a}f_{2}\|_{L_{x}^{q}},

where
\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{q}=\frac{1}{2}
.

Lemma 2.7
.

[
25
]

Let
a\geq 0
. For any scalar function
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
, we have

\|f_{1}f_{2}\|_{H_{x}^{a}}\lesssim\|f_{1}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|f_{2}\|_{H_{x}^{a}}+\|f_{2}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|f_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{a}}.

Lemma 2.8
.

[
6
]

Let
F(u)
be a smooth function of
u
,
F(0)=0
and
u\in L^{\infty}_{x}
. For any
s\geq 0
, we have

\|F(u)\|_{H^{s}}\lesssim\|u\|_{H^{s}}(1+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}).

Lemma 2.9
.

[
47
]

Suppose that
0\leq r,r^{\prime}<\frac{3}{2}
and
r+r^{\prime}>\frac{3}{2}
. Then for any scalar function
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
, the following estimate holds:

\|f_{1}f_{2}\|_{H^{r+r^{\prime}-\frac{3}{2}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq C_{r,r^{\prime}}\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|f_{2}\|_{H^{r^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

If
-r\leq r^{\prime}\leq r
and
r>\frac{3}{2}
then

\|f_{1}f_{2}\|_{H^{r^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq C_{r,r^{\prime}}\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|f_{2}\|_{H^{r^{\prime}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Lemma 2.10
.

[
59
]

Let
0\leq\alpha<1
. Then

\|\Lambda_{x}^{\alpha}(f_{1}f_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim\|f_{1}\|_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{\infty,2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|f_{2}\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Lemma 2.11
.

[
59
]

Let
0<\alpha<1
. Then

\|\Lambda_{x}^{\alpha}(f_{1}f_{2}f_{3})\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim\|f_{i}\|_{H^{1+\alpha}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\textstyle{\prod_{j\neq i}}\|f_{j}\|_{H^{1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Lemma 2.12
.

[
42
]

Given
p\in[1,\infty]

such that
p\geq m^{\prime}
with
m^{\prime}
the conjugate exponent
of
m
. Let
f,g
and
\Phi
belong to the suitable functional spaces. Then

\|\Phi*(hf)-h*(\Phi f)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|x\Phi\|_{L^{1}}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{L^{p}}.

Lemma 2.13
.

[
32
]

Let
0<a<1
. Then, for any functions
f_{1}
and
f_{2}
, the following estimates

\|\Lambda_{x}^{a}(f_{1}f_{2})-f_{1}\Lambda_{x}^{a}f_{1}-f_{2}\Lambda_{x}^{a}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\Lambda_{x}^{a}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.

(2.56)

Remark 2.2
.

Let
0<\epsilon<\frac{1}{6}
. By Lemma
2.14
, we have

\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}(f_{1}f_{2})-f_{1}\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{2}-f_{2}\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.

(2.57)

So we get

\begin{split}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}(f_{1}f_{2})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|f_{1}\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|f_{2}\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|f_{1}\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{2}\|_{L^{q}_{x}}+\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|f_{1}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{2}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}-\epsilon}_{x}}+\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}f_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|f_{1}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|f_{2}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}+\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f_{1}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}.\end{split}

Here we take
p=\frac{3}{1-\epsilon}
and
q=\frac{6}{1+2\epsilon}
.

Take
f_{1}=\partial\mathbf{w},f_{2}=\partial\mathbf{v}
,

\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}(\partial\mathbf{w}\partial\mathbf{v})\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}.

(2.58)

Similarly, if we take
f_{1}=\partial\mathbf{w},f_{2}=\partial\mathbf{v}
or
\partial\rho
, then

\begin{split}&\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}(\partial^{2}h\partial\mathbf{v})\lesssim\|\partial^{2}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial^{2}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}},\\
&\|\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}(\partial^{2}h\partial\rho)\lesssim\|\partial^{2}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\partial\rho\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial^{2}h\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}.\end{split}

(2.59)

2.3.
Useful lemmas

Let us introduce some useful product estimates and commutator estimates, which play a crucial role in the paper.

Lemma 2.14
.

Let the metric
g
is defined in (
1.4
). If
f(t,x;\tau)
is the solution of

\begin{cases}&\square_{g}f=0,\quad t>\tau,\\
&(f,\mathbf{T}f)|_{t=\tau}=-(F,B)(\tau,x),\end{cases}

then

V(t,x)=\int^{t}_{0}f(t,x;\tau)d\tau

is the solution of the linear wave equation

\begin{cases}&\square_{g}V=\mathbf{T}F+B,\\
&(V,\mathbf{T}V)|_{t=0}=(0,-F(0,x)).\end{cases}

Proof.

We first note
\square_{g}=-\mathbf{T}\mathbf{T}+c^{2}_{s}\Delta
. By calculating, we get

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}V&=\int^{t}_{0}\mathbf{T}f(t,x;\tau)d\tau+f(t,x;t)\\
&=\int^{t}_{0}\mathbf{T}f(t,x;\tau)d\tau-F(t,x).\end{split}

Applying the operator
\mathbf{T}
again, we have

\begin{split}-\mathbf{T}\mathbf{T}V&=-\int^{t}_{0}\mathbf{T}\mathbf{T}f(t,x;\tau)d\tau-\mathbf{T}f(t,x;t)+\mathbf{T}F\\
&=-\int^{t}_{0}\mathbf{T}\mathbf{T}f(t,x;\tau)d\tau+B(t,x)+\mathbf{T}F.\end{split}

(2.60)

On the other hand,

c^{2}_{s}\Delta V=\int^{t}_{0}c^{2}_{s}\Delta f(t,x;\tau)d\tau.

(2.61)

Adding (
2.60
) and (
2.61
), we can derive that

\square_{g}V=\mathbf{T}F+B.

Furthermore, the function
V
satisfies

V|_{t=0}=0,\quad\mathbf{T}V|_{t=0}=-F(0,x).

The proof of the lemma is now complete.
∎

Lemma 2.15
.

Let
0<\alpha<1
. Let
\beta>\alpha
and sufficiently close to
\alpha
. Then

\|f_{1}f_{2}\|_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{\infty,2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim\|f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f_{1}\|_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{\infty,2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|f_{2}\|_{C^{\beta}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

(2.62)

Proof.

Firstly, we have

\|f_{1}f_{2}\|^{2}_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{\infty,2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}=\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}(f_{1}f_{2})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}.

By using Bony decomposition, we get

\Delta_{j}(f_{1}f_{2})=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}f_{2}S_{k-1}f_{1})+\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}f_{1}S_{k-1}f_{2})+\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}f_{2}\Delta_{k}f_{1}).

By Hölder’s inequality, we derive that

\begin{split}&\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}(f_{1}f_{2})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim&\ \textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}(\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|S_{k-1}f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{2}\\
&\ +\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}(\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|S_{k-1}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{2}\\
&\ +\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}(\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f_{1}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{k}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{2}\\
\lesssim&\ \textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}+\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{\dot{B}^{\alpha}_{\infty,2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}\|\Delta_{j}f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}(\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}2^{2(-\beta+\alpha)}2^{k\beta}\|\Delta_{k}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{2}.\end{split}

(2.63)

It is sufficient to verify the bounds of the last right terms on (
2.63
). Note

\begin{split}\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim&\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j(-\beta+\alpha)}2^{2j\beta}\|\Delta_{j}f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim&\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\{2^{2j(-\beta+\alpha)}\}_{l^{1}_{j}}(\{2^{j\beta}\|\Delta_{j}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\}_{l^{\infty}_{j}})^{2}\\
\lesssim&\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{\dot{B}^{\beta}_{\infty,\infty}}\lesssim\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{C^{\beta}}.\end{split}

(2.64)

We also note

\begin{split}&\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}\|\Delta_{j}f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}(\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}2^{2(-k\beta+j\alpha)}2^{k\beta}\|\Delta_{k}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{2}\\
\lesssim&\ \textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}\|\Delta_{j}f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}(\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}2^{2(-k\beta+j\alpha)}2^{k\beta}\|\Delta_{k}f_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}})^{2}\\
\lesssim&\ \|f_{2}\|^{2}_{\dot{B}^{\beta}_{\infty,\infty}}\textstyle{\sum}_{j\geq-1}2^{2j(\alpha-\beta)}\|\Delta_{j}f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\\
\lesssim&\ \|f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|f_{2}\|^{2}_{C^{\beta}}.\end{split}

(2.65)

Substituting (
2.64
) and (
2.65
) in (
2.63
), we arrive at (
2.62
).
∎

The next commutator estimate is concerns Riesz operators.

Lemma 2.16
.

Let
0\leq\alpha<1
. Denote the Riesz operator
\mathbf{R}:=\partial^{2}(-\Delta)^{-1}
. Then

\|[\mathbf{R},\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{1+\alpha}_{\infty,\infty}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{\infty,\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Proof.

By paraproduct decomposition (c.f.
[
9
]
), we have

\begin{split}\Delta_{j}[\mathbf{R},\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]f&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left[\mathbf{R}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{R}S_{k-1}f\right]\\
&\quad+\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left[\mathbf{R}(S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{R}\Delta_{k}f\right]\\
&\quad+\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\Delta_{j}\left[\mathbf{R}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{R}\Delta_{k}f\right]\\
&=B_{1}+B_{2}+B_{3},\end{split}

where

\begin{split}B_{1}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left\{\mathbf{R}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{R}S_{k-1}f\right\},\\
B_{2}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left\{\mathbf{R}(S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{R}\Delta_{k}f\right\}\\
B_{3}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\Delta_{j}\left\{\mathbf{R}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\mathbf{R}\Delta_{k}f\right\}.\end{split}

For
0\leq\alpha<1
, by Hölder’s inequality and Bernstein’s inequality, we find that

\begin{split}\{2^{j\alpha}\|B_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}&\lesssim\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}(\|\nabla\mathbf{R}S_{k-1}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\nabla S_{k-1}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})2^{j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{2}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}2^{j\alpha}2^{k}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}(\|\mathbf{R}S_{k-1}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|S_{k-1}f\|_{L^{2}})\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{1+\alpha}_{\infty,\infty}}(\|\mathbf{R}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}})\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{1+\alpha}_{\infty,\infty}}\|f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(2.66)

Due to Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\{2^{j\alpha}\|B_{3}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}&\lesssim\{2^{j\alpha}\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}2^{k}\|\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\cdot 2^{-k}(\|\nabla\Delta_{k}f\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\mathbf{R}\Delta_{k}f\|_{L^{2}})\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{\infty,\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}}.\end{split}

(2.67)

Note

B_{2}=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}[\mathbf{R},S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot]\Delta_{k}\nabla f.

By using Lemma
2.12
and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\begin{split}\{2^{j\alpha}\|B_{2}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim&\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\|x\Phi\|_{L^{1}}\|\nabla S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}2^{j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
\lesssim&\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\|\nabla S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}2^{j\alpha}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{1}_{\infty,\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}}.\end{split}

(2.68)

Here, we use the fact that
{\Phi}=\frac{x_{i}x_{j}}{|x|^{2}}2^{3j}\Psi(2^{j}x)
and
\Psi
is in Schwartz space. Gathering (
2.66
), (
2.67
) and (
2.68
) together, we have finished the proof of Lemma
2.16
.
∎

Lemma 2.17
.

Let
2<s_{1}\leq s_{2}
. Then

\{2^{(s_{1}-1)j}\|[\Delta_{j},\mathbf{T}]f\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{s_{2}}}\|f\|_{{H}^{1}}.

Proof.

We first have

[\Delta_{j},\mathbf{T}]f=[\Delta_{j},\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]f.

By using paraproduct decomposition, we have

\begin{split}[\Delta_{j},\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]f&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\big(\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}\Delta_{j}f\big)\\
&\quad+\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\big(\Delta_{j}(S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}\Delta_{j}f\big)\\
&\quad+\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\big(\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}\Delta_{j}f\big)\\
&=A_{1}+A_{2}+A_{3},\end{split}

where

\begin{split}A_{1}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\left\{\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}\Delta_{j}f\right\},\\
A_{2}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\left\{\Delta_{j}(S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}\Delta_{j}f\right\}\\
A_{3}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\left\{\Delta_{j}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}\Delta_{j}f\right\}.\end{split}

Note
\mathrm{supp}\widehat{A}_{1},\mathrm{supp}\widehat{A}_{2}\subseteq\{\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{3}:2^{j-5}\leq|\xi|\leq 2^{j+5}\}
. By Hölder’s inequality and commutator estimate, we find that

\begin{split}\{2^{j(s_{1}-1)}\|A_{2}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}&\lesssim\left\{2^{j(s_{2}-1)}\|A_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&=\left\{2^{j(s_{2}-1)}\|[\Delta_{j},S_{j-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]\Delta_{j}f\|_{L^{2}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\left\{2^{j(s_{2}-1)}\|\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{j}f\|_{L^{2}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\|\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}}.\end{split}

(2.69)

Due to Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\{2^{j(s_{1}-1)}\|A_{1}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}&\lesssim\left\{2^{j(s_{2}-1)}\|A_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&=\left\{2^{j(s_{2}-1)}\|\Delta_{j}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{6}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{2}-1}_{3,2}}\|\nabla f\|_{L^{6}}\\
&\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{s_{2}-\frac{1}{2}}}\|f\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{s_{2}}}\|f\|_{H^{1}}.\end{split}

(2.70)

We rewrite
A_{3}
by the form

A_{3}=\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}[\Delta_{j},\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]\Delta_{k}f.

By using Hölder’s inequality, we get

\begin{split}\{2^{j(s_{1}-1)}\|A_{3}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}&\lesssim\|\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{2}-1}}.\end{split}

(2.71)

Thus, we complete the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 2.18
.

Let
0<\alpha<1
. Then

\|[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x},\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]f\|_{L^{2}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}_{x}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Proof.

By using paraproduct decomposition, we have

\begin{split}\Delta_{j}[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x},\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]f&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}S_{k-1}f\right]\\
&\quad+\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}(S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}\Delta_{k}f\right]\\
&\quad+\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\Delta_{j}\left[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}\Delta_{k}f\right]\\
&=V_{1}+V_{2}+V_{3},\end{split}

where

\begin{split}V_{1}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla S_{k-1}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}S_{k-1}f\right],\\
V_{2}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}\left[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}(S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}\Delta_{k}f\right]\\
&=\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\Delta_{j}[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x},S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla]\Delta_{k}f,\\
V_{3}&=\textstyle{\sum}_{k\geq j-1}\Delta_{j}\left[\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}(\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Delta_{k}f)-\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla\Lambda^{\alpha}_{x}\Delta_{k}f\right].\end{split}

For
0<\alpha<1
, by Hölder’s inequality and Bernstein’s inequality, we can derive

\begin{split}\{\|V_{1}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}&\lesssim\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}(\|\nabla\Lambda_{x}^{\alpha}S_{k-1}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+2^{j\alpha}\|\nabla S_{k-1}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}2^{\alpha j}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\|S_{k-1}\Lambda_{x}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
&\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}}.\end{split}

(2.72)

Using Hölder’s inequality again, we have

\{\|V_{3}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim\|\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}}.

(2.73)

By using Lemma
2.12
(by setting
{\Phi}=2^{j(\alpha+3)}\Psi(2^{3}x)
,
\Psi
is in Schwartz space) and Hölder inequality, we find that

\begin{split}\{\|V_{2}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim&\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}\|x\Phi\|_{L^{1}}\|\nabla S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
\lesssim&\left\{\textstyle{\sum}_{|k-j|\leq 2}2^{j\alpha}\|\nabla S_{k-1}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
\lesssim&\|\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}}\lesssim\|\nabla\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|f\|_{\dot{H}^{\alpha}}.\end{split}

(2.74)

Gathering (
2.72
), (
2.73
), and (
2.74
) together, we have proved this lemma.
∎

Lemma 2.19
.

Let
2<s_{1}\leq s_{2}
. Let
\mathbf{g}
be a Lorentz metric and
\mathbf{g}^{00}=-1
. We have

\begin{split}\left\{2^{(s_{1}-1)j}\|[\square_{\mathbf{g}},\Delta_{j}]f\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim&\ \|df\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}}+\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|df\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}}.\end{split}

(2.75)

Proof.

Let
{R}_{j}=[\square_{\mathbf{g}},\Delta_{j}]f
and
S_{j}=\textstyle\sum_{j^{\prime}\leq j}\Delta_{j^{\prime}}
. Note
\mathbf{g}^{00}=-1
. Then we can decompose
{R}_{j}
as

{R}_{j}={E}_{j}+{A}_{j}+{G}_{j},

where

\begin{split}E_{j}=&\Delta_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i}\partial_{\alpha i}f)-S_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f),\\
A_{j}=&\left(S_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})-\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i}\right)\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f),\\
G_{j}=&\Delta_{j}\big(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta})\big)\partial_{\beta}f.\end{split}

We note

\Delta_{k}\Delta_{j}f=0,\quad\mathrm{if}\ |k-j|\geq 8.

Note again the support of
\widehat{E}_{j}
being in the set
\{\xi:2^{j-5}\leq|\xi|\leq 2^{j+10}\}
, then

\begin{split}&\|E_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\lesssim 2^{j(s_{2}-s_{1})}\|E_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

For
A_{j}
, we first have

A_{j}=\sum_{j^{\prime}>j}\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f).

By classical product estimates, we derive that

\begin{split}\|A_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}&\lesssim\sum_{j^{\prime}>j}(\|\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}+\|\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\|\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\|_{L^{\infty}}).\end{split}

(2.76)

Using Bernstein’s inequality, we can update (
2.76
) as

\begin{split}&\{2^{j(s_{1}-1)}\|A_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\\
\lesssim&\{\sum_{j^{\prime}>j}(\|\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\nabla\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\|_{L^{\infty}}2^{-j^{\prime}+j}\|\Delta_{j}(\partial f)\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}}\\
&+2^{j(s_{1}-1)}\|\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\cdot 2^{j}\|\Delta_{j}(\partial f)\|_{L^{\infty}})\}_{l^{2}_{j}}.\end{split}

We set

\begin{split}N_{1}&=\{\sum_{j^{\prime}>j}\|\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\nabla\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\|_{L^{\infty}}2^{-j^{\prime}+j}\|\Delta_{j}(\partial f)\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}}\}_{l^{2}_{j}},\\
N_{2}&=\{\sum_{j^{\prime}>j}2^{j(s_{1}-1)}\|\Delta_{j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\cdot 2^{j}\|\Delta_{j}(\partial f)\|_{L^{\infty}})\}_{l^{2}_{j}}.\end{split}

A direct calculation tells us

N_{1}\lesssim\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}},\quad N_{2}\lesssim\|df\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}.

In a result, we get

\left\{2^{(s_{1}-1)j}\|A_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}+\|df\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}.

(2.77)

For
G_{j}
, by phase decomposition, we obtain

\begin{split}\Delta_{k}G_{j}=&\textstyle{\sum_{|k-m|\leq 2}}\Delta_{k}\left(\Delta_{m}\big(\Delta_{j}(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta}))\big)S_{m-1}\partial_{\beta}f\right)\\
&+\textstyle{\sum_{|k-m|\leq 2}}\Delta_{k}\left(S_{m-1}\big(\Delta_{j}(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta}))\big)\Delta_{m}\partial_{\beta}f\right)\\
&+\Delta_{k}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{k}\big(\Delta_{j}(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta}))\big)\tilde{\Delta}_{k}\partial_{\beta}f\right),\end{split}

where
\tilde{\Delta}_{k}=\Delta_{k-1}+\Delta_{k}+\Delta_{k+1}
. Due to Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\|\Delta_{k}G_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\ \|\Delta_{k}\Delta_{j}\big(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta})\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}(\|S_{k}\partial_{\beta}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\Delta_{k}\partial_{\beta}f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\\
&+\|S_{k}\big(\Delta_{j}(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta_{k}\partial_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

By Hölder’s inequality again, we prove

\begin{split}\|G_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\Delta_{j}\big(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta})\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|df\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

In a result, we can get

\|G_{j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\lesssim\|\Delta_{j}\big(c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta})\big)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|df\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|c^{-1}_{s}\partial_{\alpha}(c_{s}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha\beta})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta_{j}\partial_{\beta}f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

\left\{2^{(s_{1}-1)j}\|G_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim\|df\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}.

(2.78)

It remains for us to give a bound for
E_{j}
. By phase decomposition, we have

\begin{split}E_{j}=&\Delta_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i}\partial_{\alpha i}f)-S_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\\
=&\Delta_{j}\left(S_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\partial_{\alpha i}f\right)-S_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\\
&+\Delta_{j}\left(\Delta_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})S_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\right)+\Delta_{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\tilde{\Delta}_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\right),\end{split}

where
\tilde{\Delta}_{j}={\Delta}_{j-1}+{\Delta}_{j}+{\Delta}_{j+1}
.
By using commutator estimates, we have

\begin{split}\|E_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}&=\|[{\Delta}_{j},{S}_{j}\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i}]\Delta_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i})f\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\Delta_{j}\left(\Delta_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})S_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\right)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\Delta_{j}\left(\tilde{\Delta}_{j}(\mathbf{g}^{\alpha i})\tilde{\Delta}_{j}(\partial_{\alpha i}f)\right)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta_{j}(df)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta_{j}(d\mathbf{g})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

Therefore, we obtain

\left\{2^{(s_{1}-1)j}\|E_{j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-s_{1}}}\right\}_{l^{2}_{j}}\lesssim\|\partial\mathbf{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}+\|\partial f\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|d\mathbf{g}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{2}-1}_{x}}.

(2.79)

By combining (
2.77
), (
2.78
), and (
2.79
), we find that (
2.75
) holds.
∎

Lemma 2.20
.

Let
(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)
be a solution of (
1.8
). Let
\mathbf{v}_{-}
be defined in (
1.12
). Let
\mathbf{Q},D
and
E
be stated in (
1.14
). Let
s\in(2,\frac{5}{2}]
and
2<s_{0}<s
. Then the following estimates

\|\mathbf{Q},D,E\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\lesssim\|d\rho,d\mathbf{v},dh\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}(\|\rho,\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|h\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}),

(2.80)

and

\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\|h\|^{3}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|^{3}_{H_{x}^{s}}+(\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}+\|h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}+}}),

(2.81)

hold. Moreover, the function
\mathbf{v}_{-}
satisfies

\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H^{s_{0}+1}}\lesssim(1+\|\rho\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}.

(2.82)

Proof.

We recall the expression of
D

\begin{split}D=&-3c_{s}^{-1}\frac{\partial c_{s}}{\partial\rho}g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha}\rho\partial_{\beta}\rho+2\textstyle{\sum_{1\leq a<b\leq 3}}\big\{\partial_{a}v^{a}\partial_{b}v^{b}-\partial_{a}v^{b}\partial_{b}v^{a}\big\}\\
&-\frac{1}{\gamma}c^{2}_{s}\partial^{a}h\partial_{a}\rho-\frac{1}{\gamma}c^{2}_{s}\partial^{a}h\partial_{a}h+c^{3}_{s}\partial_{\beta}h\partial_{\alpha}(c^{-3}_{s}g^{\alpha\beta})\end{split}

which is described in (
1.14
). Using Lemma
2.7
, we can get

\|D\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\lesssim\|d\rho\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|d\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}\|\partial v\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}.

(2.83)

Note

\begin{split}{Q^{i}}:=&-\frac{2}{\gamma}e^{\rho}c^{2}_{s}\epsilon^{i}_{ab}w^{b}\partial^{a}h-(1+c_{s}^{-1}\frac{\partial c_{s}}{\partial\rho})g^{\alpha\beta}\partial_{\alpha}{\rho}\partial_{\beta}v^{i}-\frac{1}{\gamma}c^{2}_{s}\epsilon^{iab}w_{a}\partial_{b}h\\
&-\frac{1}{2}c^{2}_{s}\partial^{a}h\partial_{a}v^{i}+\frac{2c_{s}}{\gamma}\frac{\partial c_{s}}{\partial\rho}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\partial^{i}h+c^{3}_{s}\partial_{\beta}h\partial_{\alpha}(c^{-3}_{s}g^{\alpha\beta}).\end{split}

By Lemma
2.7
, we can deduce that

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{Q}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}&\lesssim(\|d\rho\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}}+\|dh\|_{L_{x}^{\infty}})(\|d\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|dh\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}).\end{split}

(2.84)

By using (
1.8
), then we have

\|d\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|dh\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|h\|_{H_{x}^{s}}.

(2.85)

Combining (
2.85
), (
2.84
), and (
2.83
), we have proved (
2.80
). It remains for us to prove (
2.81
) and (
2.82
). By using (
1.12
) and Sobolev imbedding, and elliptic estimates, we can derive that

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\leq\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}&\lesssim(1+\|\rho\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}},\end{split}

(2.86)

and

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H^{s_{0}+1}}\lesssim(1+\|\rho\|_{H^{2}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}.\end{split}

(2.87)

Let us give a bound for
\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}
. By using (
1.12
), we get

-\Delta\mathbf{v}_{-}=\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}.

Then we have

-\Delta(\mathbf{T}v_{-}^{i})=\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i})-\Delta v^{m}\partial_{m}v_{-}^{i}-2\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial^{j}(\partial_{m}v_{-}^{i}).

(2.88)

By (
2.88
), (
2.6
), and Lemma
2.9
, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{T}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim&\|\partial\mathbf{v}\cdot\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}\cdot\partial\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}_{-}\cdot\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial^{2}\rho\cdot\partial h\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
&+\|\partial h\cdot\partial\rho\cdot\partial h\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial\rho\cdot\partial\rho\cdot\partial h\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial\rho\cdot\partial^{2}h\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\\
&+(\|\partial h\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}})\|\partial\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial h\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\\
&+\|\partial\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial^{2}h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|\partial^{2}\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\|\partial h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\\
\lesssim&(\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}+\|h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}+}})(\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s}})+\|h\|^{3}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|^{3}_{H_{x}^{s}}.\end{split}

(2.89)

This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

3.
Energy estimates

In this part, we will prove the energy estimates. Let us first use the hyperbolic system to give a classical energy estimate for density, velocity, and entropy.

Theorem 3.1

(
[
37
]
)
.

(Classical energy estimates for velocity and density)
Let
(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)
be a solution of (
1.8
). Then for any
a\geq 0
, we have
and

\|({\rho},\mathbf{v},h)\|_{H_{x}^{a}}\leq\|({\rho}_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0},h_{0})\|_{H_{x}^{a}}\exp(C{\int^{t}_{0}}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau),\quad t\in[0,T].

(3.1)

3.1.
Energy estimates of Theorem
1.4

Theorem 3.2
.

(Energy estimates: type 1)
Let
(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)
be a solution of (
1.8
). Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.5
) and
\mathbf{w}
satisfy the equation (
2.4
). For
2<s_{0}<s\leq\frac{5}{2}
, the following energy estimates hold:

\begin{split}E_{s}(t)\leq&C{E}_{0}\exp\left\{E_{0}\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}d\tau\cdot\exp(\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\right\},\end{split}

(3.2)

where
C
is a universal constant in the sense of section
1.4
and

E_{s}(t)=\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}},

(3.3)

and
{E}_{0}=E_{s}(0)+E^{2}_{s}(0)+E^{3}_{s}(0)+E^{2s_{0}-1}_{s}(0)+E^{1+\frac{s_{0}-1}{s_{0}-2}}_{s}(0)
.

Proof.

By using (
3.1
), we have

\|({\rho},\mathbf{v},h)\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}(t)\leq C\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0},h_{0})\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}.

(3.4)

We then discuss
\mathbf{w}
and
h
into several steps.

Step 1: The estimate for
\mathbf{w}
. Recall that
\mathbf{w}
satisfies (
2.4
). Multiplying
\mathbf{w}
on (
2.4
) and integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we can get

\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}(t)-\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}(0)\leq C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial h\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.

(3.5)

By elliptic estimates, so we have

\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\leq\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}.

(3.6)

Let us first estimate
\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}
. Note (
2.5
). By applying Lemma
2.9
to (
2.5
), it follows that

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}&=\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\partial\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\leq C\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.7)

Let us next estimate
\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}
. Taking derivatives
\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}
of (
2.7
), we find

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\left(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+F^{i}\right)\right\}=&\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)+\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}K^{i}\\
&+[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}]\left(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+F^{i}\right).\end{split}

(3.8)

Multiplying
\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+F^{i})

on (
3.8
), and then integrating it over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, which can implies

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}(t)\lesssim&\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}(0)+|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot|\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F})|^{2}dxd\tau|\\
&+|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}]\left(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+F^{i}\right)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+F_{i})dxd\tau|\\
&+|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+F_{i})dxd\tau|\\
&+|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}K^{i}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+F_{i})dxd\tau|,\end{split}

(3.9)

where
\mathbf{F}=(F^{1},F^{2},F^{3})^{\mathrm{T}}
. By Hölder’s inequality, it follows that

\|\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}(t)\lesssim\|\rho\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}}.

(3.10)

By Young’s inequality, we further derive that

\|\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}(t)\leq\frac{1}{8}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})+C(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{4}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{4}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}).

(3.11)

For the right hand side of (
3.9
), let us set

\begin{split}I_{0}=&\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}(0),\\
I_{1}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot|\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F})|^{2}dxd\tau,\\
I_{2}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}K^{i}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+F_{i})dxd\tau,\\
I_{3}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}]\left(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+F^{i}\right)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+F_{i})dxd\tau\\
I_{4}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+F_{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

(3.12)

Let us estimate
I_{0},I_{1},\ldots,I_{4}
one by one. For
I_{0}
, we have

\begin{split}|I_{0}|&\leq\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+C(\|\rho\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})\\
&\leq\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+C(\|\rho_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}}).\end{split}

(3.13)

For
I_{1}
, using (
3.11
), we have

\begin{split}|I_{1}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\{\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\right\}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.14)

For
I_{2}
, recalling (
2.9
) and using Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.11
, we find that

\begin{split}|{I_{2}}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{K}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{w}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\partial\rho\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial^{2}\rho\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\partial^{3}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial^{2}h\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

Therefore, we get

\begin{split}|{I_{2}}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial h\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}})(\|\rho\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|h\|_{H^{2}_{x}})\\
&\qquad\quad\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial h\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}})\\
&\qquad\quad\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial h\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}})(\|\rho\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|h\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}})\\
&\qquad\quad\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.15)

Above, we set
\mathbf{K}=(K^{1},K^{2},K^{3})^{\mathrm{T}}
. For
I_{3}
, using Lemma
2.18
, we obtain

\begin{split}|I_{3}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.16)

For
I_{4}
, we divide it into
I_{4}=I_{41}+I_{42}+I_{43}+I_{44}
, where

I_{41}=2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i})dxd\tau,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \

\begin{split}I_{42}=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}\rho\partial_{i}w_{a})dxd\tau\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\rho\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k})dxd\tau,\end{split}

I_{43}=4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \

I_{44}=-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})dxd\tau,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \

Integrating
I_{41}
by parts, we find that

\begin{split}I_{41}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i})dxd\tau=0,\end{split}

(3.17)

where we use the fact
\partial^{i}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i})=\mathrm{div}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w})=0
. Due to the Plancherel formula, it follows that

\begin{split}I_{42}=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\Lambda_{x}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial^{a}\rho\partial_{i}w_{a})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\Lambda_{x}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\rho\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k})dxd\tau.\end{split}

By Hölder’s inequality again, we can bound
I_{42}
as

\begin{split}|I_{42}|&\leq C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}}\|\partial\rho\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}}d\tau\\
&\leq Ct\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{2}}.\end{split}

(3.18)

Integrating
I_{43}
by parts and using
\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\partial_{j}v^{m}=0
, gives

\begin{split}I_{43}=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau\\
=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(-\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{i}\rho\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau\\
&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau\\
&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial^{i}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau\\
=&4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau\\
&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial^{i}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}h)dxd\tau.\end{split}

Hence, by using Lemma
2.10
, we can estimate
I_{43}
by

\begin{split}|I_{43}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\left(\|\partial\rho\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{3}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\right)d\tau\\
\leq&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\rho\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{2}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.19)

Integrating
I_{44}
by parts, using
\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\partial_{k}h=0
and
\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\Delta\partial^{i}v_{j}=\Delta\mathbf{w}^{k}
, we have

\begin{split}I_{44}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{i}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})dxd\tau\\
=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(-\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{i}\rho\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{k}h\Delta\partial^{i}v_{j})dxd\tau\\
=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{k}h\Delta\mathbf{w}_{k})dxd\tau.\end{split}

(3.20)

To give a bound for
I_{44}
, let us calculate
\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j})
. The Hodge decomposition implies that

\Delta v_{j}=\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{a}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}^{b}+\partial_{j}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}=\epsilon_{jab}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial^{a}\rho w^{b}+\epsilon_{jab}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial^{a}w^{b}+\partial_{j}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}.

(3.21)

Due to (
3.21
), we obtain

\begin{split}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\Delta v_{j}=&\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}\rho w^{b}+\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}w^{b}\\
&+\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial_{j}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\\
=&\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}\rho w^{b}+\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}w^{b}\\
&+\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v})-(-\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v})\\
&-(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v})-(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}\partial_{k}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v})\\
=&\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}\rho w^{b}+\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}w^{b}\\
&+\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}).\end{split}

(3.22)

Using (
3.22
) in (
3.20
), we have

\begin{split}I_{44}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}\rho w^{b}\big)dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\epsilon_{jab}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\partial^{a}w^{b}\big)dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\left\{\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\epsilon_{i}^{\ jk}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{k}h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v})\right\}dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial_{k}h\Delta\mathbf{w}_{k})dxd\tau.\end{split}

(3.23)

Using the Plancherel formula and the Hölder inequality, we can bound
I_{44}
by

\begin{split}|I_{44}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial\rho\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\partial h\partial^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v},\rho,h\|^{3}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v},\rho,h\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|\mathbf{v},\rho,h\|_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.24)

Combining (
3.17
) to (
3.24
), we get
