\begin{split}|I_{4}|\leq&C\int^{t}_{0}(\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{3}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\cdot(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}(\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+Ct\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{2}}.\end{split}

(3.25)

On the other hand, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\leq&2\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+2\|\mathbf{F}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.26)

Combining (
3.26
), (
3.11
), (
3.14
)-(
3.25
), we can conclude that

\begin{split}&\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}(t)\\
\leq&C(\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})\\
&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+Ct\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{2}}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.27)

Adding (
3.27
) with (
3.7
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}(t)\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+Ct\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{2}}\\
&+C(\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.28)

By interpolation, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{1}{2(s_{0}-1)}}_{L_{x}^{2}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{1-\frac{1}{2(s_{0}-1)}}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}},\\
\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{2}}\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{s_{0}-2}{s_{0}-1}}_{H_{x}^{1}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{1}{s_{0}-1}}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}},\\
\|h\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\lesssim&\|h\|^{\frac{1}{2(s_{0}-1)}}_{H_{x}^{2}}\|h\|^{1-\frac{1}{2(s_{0}-1)}}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.29)

By using (
3.29
) and Young’s inequality, we can derive that
2
2

2

In (
3.30
), we get the highest-derivatives norm
\frac{1}{100}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}
for it will be cancelled by energy term
\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}
.

\begin{split}Ct\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{2}}\leq&Ct\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{2(s_{0}-2)}{s_{0}-1}}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{1}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{\frac{2}{s_{0}-1}}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\\
\leq&Ct^{1+\frac{1}{s_{0}-2}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2+\frac{s_{0}-1}{s_{0}-2}}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|^{\frac{s_{0}-1}{s_{0}-2}}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}+\frac{1}{100}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}.\end{split}

(3.30)

In a similar way, we find that

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|h\|^{\frac{1}{s_{0}-1}}_{H_{x}^{2}}\|h\|^{2-\frac{1}{s_{0}-1}}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{v}\|^{4(s_{0}-1)}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|h\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{2}}+\frac{1}{100}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}},\end{split}

(3.31)

and

\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}\leq C\|\rho\|^{4(s_{0}-1)}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}+\frac{1}{100}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}.

(3.32)

Using (
3.30
)-(
3.32
), (
3.28
) yields, upon cancelling the small terms with factor
1/100
from (
3.30
), (
3.32
),

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}(t)\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+Ct^{1+\frac{1}{s_{0}-2}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2+\frac{s_{0}-1}{s_{0}-2}}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\rho\|^{\frac{s_{0}-1}{s_{0}-2}}_{L_{t}^{\infty}{H}_{x}^{s_{0}}}+\frac{1}{50}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\\
&+C\|\mathbf{v}\|^{4(s_{0}-1)}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|h\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{2}}+C\|\rho\|^{4(s_{0}-1)}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L_{t}^{\infty}H_{x}^{s_{0}}}+\frac{1}{100}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.33)

Step 2: The estimate for
h
.

By using the equation (
1.8
), we can see that

\partial_{t}h+(\mathbf{v}\cdot\nabla)h=0.

This gives the
L^{2}
estimate

\|h(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.

(3.34)

The Gronwall’s inequality tells us

\|h(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq C\|h_{0}\|_{L^{2}}\exp\big({\int^{t}_{0}}\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\big).

(3.35)

For the high-order energy, noticing

\|\Delta h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}=\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}+\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}},

we have

\|\Delta h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\leq\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+\|\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}+2\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}\|\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}.

(3.36)

Applying
\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}
to (
2.48
), we get

\mathbf{T}\left\{\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}=-2\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}[\partial^{k}(\partial_{m}v^{j})\partial_{j}H^{m}]+\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}Y-[\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2},\mathbf{T}]\partial^{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}).

(3.37)

Multiplying (
3.37
) by
\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})
integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\begin{split}\|(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(t)=&\|(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(0)+\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot|\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\nabla(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})|^{2}dxd\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}Y^{k}\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2},\mathbf{T}]\partial^{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}[\partial^{k}(\partial_{m}v^{j})\partial_{j}H^{m}]\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&\widetilde{I}_{0}+\widetilde{I}_{1}+\widetilde{I}_{2}+\widetilde{I}_{3}+\widetilde{I}_{4},\end{split}

(3.38)

where

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{0}=&\|(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(0),\\
\widetilde{I}_{1}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot|\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\nabla(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})|^{2}dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{2}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}Y^{k}\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{3}=&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2},\mathbf{T}]\partial^{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{4}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}[\partial^{k}(\partial_{m}v^{j})\partial_{j}H^{m}]\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

Let us estimate
\widetilde{I}_{0},\widetilde{I}_{1},\ldots,\widetilde{I}_{4}
one by one. Note

\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}=\Delta h-\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h.

(3.39)

By using Hölder’s inequality and (
3.39
), we can estimate the term
I_{0}
by

|\tilde{I}_{0}|\leq\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}}+C\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}.

(3.40)

For the term
\widetilde{I}_{1}
, we can estimate it by

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{1}|\leq&C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.41)

By using Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.11
and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{2}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.42)

For
\widetilde{I}_{3}
, using Lemma
2.18
, we have

|\widetilde{I}_{3}|\lesssim\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}d\tau.

(3.43)

It remains for us to handle the difficult term
\widetilde{I}_{4}
, which is the most difficult term. To estimate it, let us integrate it by parts as follows:

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{4}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}[\partial^{k}(\partial_{m}v^{j})\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h]\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}[\partial^{k}v^{j}\partial_{m}(\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)]\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{k}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&\widetilde{I}_{40}+\widetilde{I}_{41},\end{split}

(3.44)

where

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{40}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}[\partial^{k}v^{j}\partial_{m}(\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)]\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{41}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{k}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

By Lemma
2.10
and (
3.39
), it’s easy for us to obtain

|\widetilde{I}_{40}|\lesssim\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}d\tau.

(3.45)

However, for
\widetilde{I}_{41}
, we may not estimate it directly. We then insert a term to transfer some derivatives.

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{41}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{(\partial^{k}v^{j}-\partial^{j}v^{k})\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\\
=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{\epsilon^{lkj}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{l}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&\widetilde{I}_{42}+\widetilde{I}_{43}.\end{split}

(3.46)

Above, we set

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{42}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{\epsilon^{lkj}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{l}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{43}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

For
\widetilde{I}_{42}
, we can integrate it by parts in the following

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{42}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\{\epsilon^{lkj}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{l}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial^{2}_{mj}\{\epsilon^{lkj}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{l}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}\{\epsilon^{lkj}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{l}\partial_{j}\rho\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

By Hölder’s inequality, we can bound
\widetilde{I}_{42}
by

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{42}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}(\|\mathbf{w}\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\partial h\partial\rho\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}})\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\{\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\{\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.47)

If we estimate
\widetilde{I}_{43}
by Sobolev inequalities, then there is a loss of derivatives. So let us integrate it by parts as follows.

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{43}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{k}(\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial_{k}\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

To write
\widetilde{I}_{43}
in a simpler form, we set

\widetilde{I}_{43}=\widetilde{I}_{44}+\widetilde{I}_{45},

(3.48)

where

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{44}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{k}\partial_{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{45}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\partial_{k}v^{k}\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

By Hölder’s inequality, we can use Lemma
2.10
to obtain

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{44}|\leq&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{3}h\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.49)

For
\widetilde{I}_{45}
, using the fact
\partial_{k}v^{k}=\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}=-\mathbf{T}\rho
yields

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{45}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\left\{\partial^{j}(\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v})\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\right\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{\partial^{j}(\mathbf{T}\rho)\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{\mathbf{T}(\partial^{j}\rho)\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{l}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

By commutators rule between the derivatives operators, we further derive that

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{45}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{T}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{l}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}](\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{(\partial^{j}\rho\mathbf{T}(\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\mathbf{T}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}]\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\}dxd\tau.\end{split}

For simplicity, we also write

\widetilde{I}_{45}=\widetilde{I}_{46}+\widetilde{I}_{47}+\widetilde{I}_{48}+\widetilde{I}_{49}+\widetilde{I}_{410}+\widetilde{I}_{411}.

(3.50)

Here, we set

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{46}&=2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{T}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{47}&=2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}v^{l}\partial_{l}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{48}&=-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}](\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{49}&=-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\{(\partial^{j}\rho\mathbf{T}(\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{410}&=2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\left\{\mathbf{T}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}dxd\tau,\\
\widetilde{I}_{411}&=-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}]\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\}dxd\tau.\end{split}

(3.51)

Integrating
\widetilde{I}_{46}
by parts, we have

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{46}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{T}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\right\}dxd\tau\\
=&2\int^{t}_{0}\frac{d}{d\tau}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau\\
=&M(t)-M(0)+\widetilde{I}_{412},\end{split}

(3.52)

where

\begin{split}M(t)=&2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dx,\\
\widetilde{I}_{412}=&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

Using Sobolev’s inequality, we find

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{412}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.53)

By Lemma
2.10
, we also have

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{47}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.54)

By utilizing Lemma
2.18
and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{48}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}(\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.55)

We note that
\mathbf{T}(\partial^{2}h)
satisfies

\mathbf{T}(\partial^{2}h)=-\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}\cdot\partial h+\partial\mathbf{v}\cdot\partial^{2}h.

Hence, by Hölder’s inequality again, we can estimate
\tilde{I}_{49}
by

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{49}|\leq&C\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\partial h\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}})\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|(\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\mathbf{v}\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\rho\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|(\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.56)

To estimate
\widetilde{I}_{410}
, we calculate

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{410}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\left\{-2\partial_{m}(\partial_{i}v^{j})\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+Y_{m}\right\}dxd\tau\\
=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\left\{\partial_{m}(\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h)\right\}dxd\tau\\
&+4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{m}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h)dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}Y_{m}dxd\tau.\end{split}

By the Plancherel formula, we have

\begin{split}\widetilde{I}_{410}=&2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\left\{-2\partial_{m}(\partial_{i}v^{j})\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h+Y_{m}\right\}dxd\tau\\
=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}}_{x}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{s_{0}-2}\Lambda^{-\frac{1}{2}}_{x}\left\{\partial_{m}(\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h)\right\}dxd\tau\\
&+4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{m}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h)dxd\tau\\
&+2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}(\partial^{j}\rho\partial_{j}\partial^{m}h)\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}Y_{m}dxd\tau.\end{split}

Using (
2.48
) and Hölder’s inequality, we deduce that

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{410}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{3}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{Y}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}})\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\{\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{3}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\{\|(\mathbf{v},\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.57)

By Lemma
2.18
, we also have

\begin{split}|\widetilde{I}_{411}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-2}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|\Delta h-\partial\rho\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{0}_{\infty,2}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.58)

Observing form (
3.38
), (
3.44
), (
3.46
), (
3.48
), (
3.50
), and (
3.51
), we have

\begin{split}\|(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(t)=&M(t)-M(0)+\widetilde{I}_{0}+\widetilde{I}_{1}+\widetilde{I}_{2}+\widetilde{I}_{3}+\widetilde{I}_{40}+\widetilde{I}_{42}\\
&+\widetilde{I}_{44}+\widetilde{I}_{47}+\widetilde{I}_{48}+\widetilde{I}_{49}+\widetilde{I}_{410}+\widetilde{I}_{411}+\widetilde{I}_{412}.\end{split}

(3.59)

By using (
3.59
) and then combining (
3.13
), (
3.41
), (
3.42
), (
3.16
), (
3.45
), (
3.47
), (
3.49
), (
3.54
), (
3.55
), (
3.56
), (
3.57
), (
3.58
) and (
3.53
), we can conclude that

\begin{split}&\|(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(t)\\
\lesssim&M(t)-M(0)+\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}}+\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}})\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.60)

By Hölder’s inequality, we have

|M(t)|\lesssim\|\rho\|_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|h\|_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}}.

(3.61)

Using Young’s inequality, (
3.61
) yields

|M(t)|\leq\frac{1}{100}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+C\|\rho\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}^{2}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}.

(3.62)

Combining (
3.60
) with (
3.62
), we obtain that

\begin{split}&\|(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}(t)\\
\lesssim&\|\rho\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}^{2}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}}+\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}+\frac{1}{100}\|h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}}d\tau+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}})\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}(\|h\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}+1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.63)

Step 3: The total energy.
By (
3.1
), we have

\|(h,\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\leq C\|(h_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}.

(3.64)

Gathering (
3.4
), (
3.5
), (
3.33
), (
3.34
), (
3.36
), (
3.63
), (
3.64
) and taking
0<t\leq\frac{1}{2C}
, upon cancelling the small terms with factor
1/100
,
1/50
from (
3.33
), (
3.63
), we have

\begin{split}E_{s}(t)\leq&CE_{0}\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}(\|(h_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\\
&+\|(h_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}+\|(h_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{3}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}})\cdot\exp\{\int^{\tau}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}E_{s}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(h_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{s_{0}}_{x}}\exp\{\int^{\tau}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}E_{s}(\tau)d\tau,\end{split}

(3.65)

where
{E}_{0}=E_{s}(0)+E^{2}_{s}(0)+E^{3}_{s}(0)+E^{2s_{0}-1}_{s}(0)+E^{1+\frac{s_{0}-1}{s_{0}-2}}_{s}(0)
.
By using Gronwall’s equality, we conclude that

\begin{split}E_{s}(t)\leq&C{E}_{0}\exp\left\{{E}_{0}\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}d\tau\cdot\exp(\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\right\}.\end{split}

So we have proved Theorem
3.2
.
∎

3.2.
Energy estimates of Theorem
1.6

Theorem 3.3
.

Let
(\rho,\mathbf{v})
be a solution of (
1.8
) with
h=0
. Let
2<s<\frac{5}{2}
. Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.4
). Then the following energy estimates hold:

\begin{split}\mathcal{E}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{0}\exp\{\mathcal{E}_{1}\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\cdot\exp(\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\},\end{split}

(3.66)

where
\mathcal{E}(t)
,
\mathcal{E}_{0}
and
\mathcal{E}_{1}
are defined by

\mathcal{E}(t)=\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{2}},

(3.67)

and

\begin{split}\mathcal{E}_{0}=&\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{2}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{s}},\end{split}

(3.68)

and

\mathcal{E}_{1}=1+\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|_{H^{2}}+\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{3}_{H^{2}}.

(3.69)

Proof.

By using (
3.1
), taking
h=0
and integrating over
[0,t]
, we have

\|({\rho},\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}(t)\leq\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|({\rho},\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}d\tau.

(3.70)

We then discuss
\mathbf{w}
into several steps.

Step 1: The estimate for
\mathbf{w}
. Recall that
\mathbf{w}
satisfies (
2.4
). Taking
h=0
, (
2.4
) and (
2.7
) reduce to

\mathbf{T}w^{i}=(\mathbf{w}\cdot\nabla)v^{i},

(3.71)

and

\begin{split}&\mathbf{T}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+\Omega^{i})=\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)+\Gamma^{i},\end{split}

(3.72)

where

\Omega^{i}=-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\rho\cdot\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k}-2\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a},

(3.73)

and

\begin{split}\Gamma^{i}=\Gamma^{i}_{1}+\Gamma^{i}_{2}+\Gamma^{i}_{3}+\Gamma^{i}_{4},\end{split}

(3.74)

and

\begin{split}\Gamma^{i}_{1}=&-2\epsilon_{kmn}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial^{m}v^{a}\partial_{j}(\partial^{n}w_{a})+2\epsilon_{kmn}\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{m}v^{a}\partial_{n}w_{a}\partial_{j}{\rho},\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \end{split}

\begin{split}\Gamma^{i}_{2}=&-2\partial^{a}{\rho}\big(\partial^{i}w^{m}\partial_{m}v_{a}+w^{m}\partial^{i}(\partial_{m}v_{a})-\partial^{i}v^{k}\partial_{k}w_{a}\big)\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \\
&-2\partial^{a}v^{k}\partial_{k}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{a}\partial^{i}w_{a}+2\epsilon^{ajk}\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}w_{k}\partial_{j}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a},\end{split}

\Gamma^{i}_{3}=2\partial^{i}{\rho}\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}w_{a}-2\partial_{j}v^{a}\partial^{j}\partial^{i}w_{a}-\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k}),\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \

\begin{split}\Gamma^{i}_{4}=&2\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}-\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}(\mathrm{e}^{-\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k})+2\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot\partial^{a}{\rho}\partial^{i}w_{a}.\end{split}

Multiplying (
3.71
) bt
\mathbf{w}
and integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we can get

\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}(t)-\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}(0)\leq C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.

(3.75)

By elliptic estimates, we have

\|\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}\leq\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}.

(3.76)

Let us first estimate
\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-1}_{x}}
. Note (
2.18
). Using product estimates in Lemma
2.9
, we can derive that

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{1}_{x}}&=\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\partial\rho\|_{{H}^{1}_{x}}\leq C\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.77)

Let us next estimate
\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}
. Multiplying (
3.72
) by
\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}+\Omega^{i}
on , and integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we find

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(t)\leq&\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(0)+C|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega|^{2}dxd\tau|\\
&+C|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+\Omega_{i})dxd\tau|\\
&+C|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma^{i}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+\Omega_{i})dxd\tau|,\end{split}

(3.78)

where
\Omega=(\Omega^{1},\Omega^{2},\Omega^{3})
and
\Gamma=(\Gamma^{1},\Gamma^{2},\Gamma^{3})
. By Hölder’s inequality, we can estimate

\|\Omega\|_{L^{2}_{x}}(t)\leq C\|\rho\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}.

(3.79)

By Young’s inequality, we further derive that

\|\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(t)\leq\frac{1}{8}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+C\|\rho\|^{4}_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(3.80)

For the right hand side of (
3.78
), let us set

\begin{split}G_{0}=&\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(0),\\
G_{1}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\cdot|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega|^{2}dxd\tau,\\
G_{2}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Gamma^{i}\cdot(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+\Omega_{i})dxd\tau,\\
G_{3}=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{i}\big(2\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}+\Omega_{i})dxd\tau.\end{split}

(3.81)

Let us estimate
G_{0},G_{1},\ldots,G_{4}
one by one. For
G_{0}
, we can use Hölder’s inequality to bound it by

\begin{split}|G_{0}|&\leq\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\|\rho_{0}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}\leq C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+C\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.82)

For
G_{1}
, we have

\begin{split}|G_{1}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\{\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\right\}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.83)

For
G_{2}
, recalling the expression of (
2.9
) and using Lemma
2.10
, we can obtain

\begin{split}|{G_{2}}|\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}\|\Gamma\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
\lesssim&\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|\rho\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{2}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|\rho\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.84)

For
G_{4}
, we divide it into
G_{4}=G_{41}+G_{42}
. Here

G_{41}=2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i})dxd\tau,\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \

\begin{split}G_{42}=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)(\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}}\partial^{a}\rho\partial_{i}w_{a})dxd\tau\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)(\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\rho\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k})dxd\tau.\end{split}

Integrating
G_{41}
by parts, we have

G_{41}=-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\partial^{i}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i})dxd\tau=0.

(3.85)

Here we use the fact
\partial^{i}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i})=\mathrm{div}(\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w})=0
. Due to the Plancherel formula, we have

\begin{split}G_{42}=&-4\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial^{a}\rho\partial_{i}w_{a})dxd\tau\\
&-2\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda_{x}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\partial^{i}\big(\partial_{n}v_{a}\partial^{n}w^{a}\big)\cdot\Lambda_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\epsilon^{ijk}\partial_{j}\rho\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{k})dxd\tau.\end{split}

We then use Hölder’s inequality to bound
G_{42}

\begin{split}|G_{42}|&\leq C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\partial\rho\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}}}d\tau\\
&\leq Ct\sup_{\tau\in[0,t]}(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{{s}}}(\tau)+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{2}}(\tau)+\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}_{x}^{{s}}}(\tau)).\end{split}

(3.86)

On the other hand, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}+\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+2\|\Omega\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.87)

Combining (
3.82
), (
3.83
), (
3.84
), (
3.85
)-(
3.86
), we get the following estimate

\begin{split}&\|\mathrm{curl}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}(t)\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}}+C\|\rho_{0}\|_{H^{{s}}}\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\\
&+Ct(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}_{x}^{{s}}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}_{x}^{2}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}_{x}^{{s}}})\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{{s}}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.88)

Adding (
3.88
) with (
3.7
), we can prove

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}(t)\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+C\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{{s}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}\\
&+Ct(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}_{x}^{{s}}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}_{x}^{2}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}{H}_{x}^{{s}}})\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}})d\tau.\end{split}

(3.89)

By interpolation formula and Young’s inequality, we can derive that

\begin{split}C\|\rho\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{{s}}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}\|\rho\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{{s}}_{x}}+\frac{1}{16}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{{s}}}\exp(6\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v},d\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)+\frac{1}{16}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.90)

Gathering (
3.70
), (
3.75
), (
3.89
) and (
3.90
), and taking

0<t\leq\frac{1}{10(C+1)},

(3.91)

we have

\begin{split}\mathcal{E}(t)\leq&C\mathcal{E}(0)+C\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{4}_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{{s}}_{x}}\exp(6\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v},d\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\mathcal{E}(\tau)d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\mathcal{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}\mathcal{E}(\tau)d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}}\mathcal{E}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

By Gronwall’s inequality, we conclude that

\begin{split}\mathcal{E}(t)\leq&\{C\mathcal{E}(0)+C\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{{s}}}\exp(6\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v},d\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\}F(t),\end{split}

(3.92)

where

F(t)=\exp\{C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(\rho,\mathbf{v})\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}})d\tau\}.

By using (
3.70
), we have

\begin{split}F(t)\leq&\exp\{C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau(1+\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\\
&\quad+\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{3}_{H^{2}_{x}})\exp(C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\}.\end{split}

(3.93)

Combining (
3.92
) and (
3.93
), we can obtain (
3.66
).
∎

Corollary 3.4
.

Let
(\rho,\mathbf{v})
be a solution of (
1.8
) with
h=0
. Let
2<s<\frac{5}{2}
. Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.4
). Then the following energy estimates hold:

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}(t)\lesssim\mathbb{E}_{0}\exp\{\mathbb{E}_{1}\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\cdot\exp(\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\},\end{split}

(3.94)

where
\mathbb{E}(t)
,
\mathbb{E}_{0}
and
\mathbb{E}_{1}
are defined by

\begin{split}\mathbb{E}(t)=&\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{3}},\\
\mathbb{E}_{0}=&\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{3}}+\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{s}}\|\rho_{0}\|^{4}_{H^{s}},\\
\mathbb{E}_{1}=&(1+\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{3}}+\|\rho_{0}\|_{H^{3}}).\end{split}

Proof.

By using (
3.1
), we have

\|({\rho},\mathbf{v})\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}(t)\leq C\|(\rho_{0},\mathbf{v}_{0})\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}.

(3.95)

Multiplying (
3.71
) by
\mathbf{w}
and integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we get

\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}\leq\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{L_{x}^{2}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.

(3.96)

Let us first estimate
\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}
. Note (
2.18
). Using product estimates in Lemma
2.9
, we can derive that

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{2}_{x}}&=\|\mathbf{w}\cdot\partial\rho\|_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\leq C\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}+}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{s+1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.97)

Let us next estimate
\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{2}_{x}}
. By using (
2.17
), we obtain

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}(\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i})=&-\epsilon^{ijk}\Delta(\partial_{j}v^{m}\partial_{m}w_{k})+\epsilon^{ijk}\Delta(\partial_{m}v_{k}\partial_{j}w^{m})+\Delta\{w^{m}\partial_{m}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w^{i})\}\\
&-\Delta v^{m}\partial_{m}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}-\partial^{k}v^{m}\partial_{k}\partial_{m}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{i}\end{split}

(3.98)

Multiplying (
3.98
) by
\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{i}
and integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}-\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{3}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}\partial(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathbf{w})\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.99)

Using Hölder’s inequality, (
3.99
) yields

\begin{split}\|\Delta\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\rho\|_{H^{3}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.100)

Due to (
3.97
), (
3.100
) and Young’s inequality, we can show that

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{3}_{x}}\leq&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{s+1}}\|\rho\|^{4}_{{H}^{s+1}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}})d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{3}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(3.101)

Using (
3.95
), (
3.96
), (
3.101
), and Gronwall’s inequality, we get (
3.94
).
∎

3.3.
Energy estimates of Theorem
1.5

Theorem 3.5
.

Let
(\rho,\mathbf{v},h)
be a solution of (
1.8
). Let
\mathbf{w}
be defined in (
1.5
) and
\mathbf{w}
satisfy the equation (
2.4
). Then the following energy estimates hold:

\begin{split}\tilde{E}(t)\leq&C\tilde{E}_{0}\exp\{\tilde{E}_{1}\int^{t}_{0}\|d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}.\end{split}

(3.102)

Here
\tilde{E}(t)
,
\tilde{E}_{0}
and
\tilde{E}_{1}
are defined by

\tilde{E}(t)=\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|\rho\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|h\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}},

(3.103)

and

\begin{split}\tilde{E}_{0}=&\tilde{E}(0)+\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\},\end{split}

(3.104)

and

\begin{split}\tilde{E}_{1}=&(\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0})\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0})\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}})\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}.\end{split}

(3.105)

Proof.

To prove (
3.102
), we divide it into three steps.

Step 1: The energy estimate for
\mathbf{w}
.
By elliptic estimates, we obtain that

\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq 2\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+2\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}.

(3.106)

Note (
2.5
). By Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality, we can bound
\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}
by

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{div}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{1+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{16}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\rho\|^{6}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.107)

On the other hand, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&2\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+2\|\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
\leq&2\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}.\end{split}

(3.108)

By Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.18
, Lemma
2.6
, Hölder’s inequality and (
2.58
), (
2.59
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\frac{d}{dt}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|(\partial\rho,\partial h)\cdot\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|(\partial\rho,\partial h,\partial\mathbf{v})\cdot(\partial\rho,\partial h,\partial\mathbf{v})\cdot(\partial\rho,\partial h,\partial\mathbf{v})\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+(\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+(\|\partial\rho\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|\partial h\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}})\|\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial h\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{W^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon,3}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{W^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon,3}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial h\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{W^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon,3}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|^{3}_{L^{6}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|^{3}_{L^{6}_{x}}+\|\partial h\|^{3}_{L^{6}_{x}})\\
\lesssim&\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}(\|h\|_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}})\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(\|h\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|_{H^{2}_{x}})\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+(\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\partial h\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})(\|\mathbf{v}\|^{4}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|\rho\|^{4}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}+\|h\|^{4}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}).\end{split}

(3.109)

Integrating (
3.109
) over
[0,t]
, we find that

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}-\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\partial\mathbf{v}+\frac{1}{\gamma}\partial h\mathrm{div}\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

The above estimate combining with (
3.108
) yields

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&C\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(3.110)

Step 2: The energy estimate for
h
.
Let us now estimate
\|\Delta h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}
. Note
\Delta h=\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}+\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h
. Thus, we have

\|\Delta h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq 2\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+2\|\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}.

(3.111)

Notice

\|\partial_{i}\rho\partial^{i}h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq C\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}.

Taking derivatives
\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}
of (
2.47
), we have

\begin{split}\mathbf{T}\{\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})\}=&-2\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}(\partial_{i}v^{j}\partial_{j}\partial^{i}h)+\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}(\epsilon^{ijm}\mathrm{e}^{\rho}w_{m}\partial^{i}\rho\partial_{j}h)+[\mathbf{T},\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}](\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}).\end{split}

(3.112)

Multiplying
\Lambda^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i})
by (
3.112
) and integrating over
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
gives

\begin{split}\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&C\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}}+C\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\cdot\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\partial^{2}h\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{w}\partial h\partial\rho\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\partial_{i}H^{i}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\rho\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}\|h\|^{2}_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\mathbf{v}\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}\|\rho\|_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(h,\rho)\|^{2}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\|h\|_{{H}^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\rho,\partial h)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(h,\rho)\|^{3}_{{H}^{2}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

Therefore, we can conclude that

\begin{split}\|\Delta h\|^{2}_{\dot{H}^{\frac{1}{2}+\epsilon}_{x}}\leq&C\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}+\epsilon}}+C\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|^{2}_{{H}^{2+\epsilon}_{x}}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau++C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(3.113)

Step 3: The total energy estimates.
By gathering (
3.107
), (
3.110
), and (
3.113
), we have

\begin{split}\tilde{E}(t)\leq&C\tilde{E}(0)+C\int^{t}_{0}\{\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}_{x}}\}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau\\
&+C\|\rho_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}\|h_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{\frac{5}{2}}}\exp\{C\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\partial\mathbf{v},\partial h,\partial\rho)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\{\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|_{H^{2}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v},h,\rho)\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{x}}\}\tilde{E}(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

By Gronwall’s inequality, we can prove that

\begin{split}\tilde{E}(t)\leq&\tilde{E}_{0}\exp\{\tilde{E}_{1}\int^{t}_{0}\|(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\},\end{split}

where
\tilde{E}_{0}
and
\tilde{E}_{1}
are stated in (
3.104
) and (
3.105
).
∎

3.4.
Uniqueness of the solution

Corollary 3.6
.

Assume
2<s_{0}<s\leq\frac{5}{2}
and (
1.27
). Suppose
(\mathbf{v}_{1},\rho_{1},h_{1})
and
(\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{2},h_{2})
to be solutions of (
1.1
) with the same initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0})\in H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{s_{0}+1}
. We assume the initial specific vorticity
\mathbf{w}_{0}=\bar{\rho}\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{0}\in H^{s_{0}}
. Then there exists a constant
T>0
such that

(\mathbf{v}_{1},\rho_{1},\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{2})\in C([0,T],H_{x}^{s})\cap C^{1}([0,T],H_{x}^{s-1})
,
\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T],H_{x}^{s_{0}})\cap C^{1}([0,T],H_{x}^{s_{0}-1})
and
(d\mathbf{v}_{1},d\rho_{1},dh_{1},d\mathbf{v}_{2},d\rho_{2},dh_{2})\in{L^{2}_{[0,T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
. Furthermore, we have

\mathbf{v}_{1}=\mathbf{v}_{2},\quad\rho_{1}=\rho_{2},\quad h_{1}=h_{2}.

Proof.

By using Lemma
2.1
and Strichartz estimates
(d\mathbf{v}_{1},d\rho_{1},dh_{1},d\mathbf{v}_{2},d\rho_{2},dh_{2})\in{L^{2}_{[0,T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
, we can bound

\|\mathbf{v}_{1}-\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathbf{v}_{1}-\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},h_{1}-h_{2}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}(0)=0.

So the solution is unique.
∎

Similarly, we can obtain the following corollary.

Corollary 3.7
.

Assume
2<s\leq\frac{5}{2}
and (
1.27
). Suppose
(\mathbf{v}_{1},\rho_{1})
and
(\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{2})
to be solutions of (
1.13
) with the same initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0})\in H^{s}\times H^{s}
. We assume the initial specific vorticity
\mathbf{w}_{0}=\bar{\rho}\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{0}\in H^{2}
. Then there exists a constant
T^{*}>0
such that

(\mathbf{v}_{1},\rho_{1},\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{2})\in C([0,T^{*}],H_{x}^{s})\cap C^{1}([0,T^{*}],H_{x}^{s-1})
,
\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}],H_{x}^{2})\cap C^{1}([0,T^{*}],H_{x}^{1})
and
(d\mathbf{v}_{1},d\rho_{1},d\mathbf{v}_{2},d\rho_{2})\in{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
. Furthermore, we have

\mathbf{v}_{1}=\mathbf{v}_{2},\quad\rho_{1}=\rho_{2}.

Corollary 3.8
.

Assume
0<\epsilon<\frac{1}{9}
and (
1.27
). Suppose
(\mathbf{v}_{1},\rho_{1},h_{1})
and
(\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{2},h_{2})
to be solutions of (
1.1
) with the same initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0})\in H^{\frac{5}{2}}\times H^{\frac{5}{2}}\times H^{\frac{5}{2}+}
. We assume the initial specific vorticity
\mathbf{w}_{0}=\bar{\rho}\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{0}\in H^{\frac{3}{2}+}
. Then there exists a constant
\bar{T}>0
such that

(\mathbf{v}_{1},\rho_{1},\mathbf{v}_{2},\rho_{2})\in C([0,\bar{T}],H_{x}^{\frac{5}{2}})\cap C^{1}([0,\bar{T}],H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}})
,
\mathbf{w}\in C([0,\bar{T}],H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+})\cap C^{1}([0,\bar{T}],H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+})
,
(d\mathbf{v}_{1},d\rho_{1},dh_{1})\in{L^{2}_{[0,\bar{T}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
, and
(d\mathbf{v}_{2},d\rho_{2},dh_{2})\in{L^{2}_{[0,\bar{T}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
. Furthermore, we have

\mathbf{v}_{1}=\mathbf{v}_{2},\quad\rho_{1}=\rho_{2},\quad h_{1}=h_{2}.

4.
Proof of Theorem
1.4
: the existence for small, smooth, compactly supported data

Theorem
1.4
includes two parts, the exisitence of solutions and the continuous dependence of solutions. Of course, we should prove the existence of solutions at first. For the continuous dependence of solutions in Theorem
1.4
, we will prove it in Section
9
as a separate part. In this section, our goal is to give a reduction of the existence of solutions in Theorem
1.4
to the case of small, smooth, compactly supported data by using physical localization arguments. To obtain our goal, we first state Proposition
4.1
for considering smooth initial data, and then we prove Theorem
1.4
by assuming Proposition
4.1
holds. After that, we introduce Proposition
4.2
, and we prove Proposition
4.1
by assuming Proposition
4.2
. So we reduce the proof of Theorem
1.4
to Proposition
4.2
, which is about the existence result for small, smooth, compactly supported data.

4.1.
A reduction to the case of smooth initial data

Proposition 4.1
.

Let
2<s_{0}<s\leq\frac{5}{2}
and (
1.27
) hold. For each
M_{0}>0
, there exists
T,M>0
(depending on
C_{0},c_{0},s,s_{0}
, and
M_{0}
) such that, for each smooth initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})
which satisfies

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}}}+\|h_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}+1}}\leq M_{0},\end{split}

(4.1)

there exists a smooth solution
(\mathbf{v},\rho,h,\mathbf{w})
to (
1.13
) on
[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfying

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v},\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}H_{x}^{s_{0}}}+\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}H_{x}^{s_{0}+1}}\leq M,\\
&\|\mathbf{v},\rho,h\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq 1+C_{0}.\end{split}

(4.2)

Furthermore, the solution satisfies the following properties:

\mathrm{(1)}
dispersive estimate for
\mathbf{v}
,
\rho
,
h
and
\mathbf{v}_{+}

\|d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh\|_{L^{2}_{t}C^{\delta}_{x}}+\|d\mathbf{v}_{+},d{\rho},d\mathbf{v},dh\|_{L^{2}_{t}\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\leq M,

(4.3)

\mathrm{(2)}
Let
f
satisfy equation. For each
1\leq r\leq s+1
, the Cauchy problem (
1.35
) is well-posed in
H_{x}^{r}\times H_{x}^{r-1}
. Moreover, the following energy estimate

\begin{split}\|{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{0}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(4.4)

and Strichartz estimate

\|\left<\partial\right>^{k}f\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}}),\quad k<r-1,

(4.5)

holds, and the same estimates hold with
\left<\partial\right>^{k}
replaced by
\left<\partial\right>^{k-1}d
. Above,
C_{M_{0}}
is a constant depending on
C_{0},c_{0},s,s_{0},M_{0}
.

In the following, we will use Proposition
4.1
to prove Theorem
1.4
.

4.2.
Proof of Theorem
1.4
by Proposition
4.1

Consider the initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\in H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{s_{0}+1}\times H^{s_{0}}
Theorem
1.4
and

\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{s}}+\|{\rho}_{0}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}}}+\|h_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}+1}}\leq M_{0},

Let
\{(\mathbf{v}_{0k},\rho_{0k},h_{0k},\mathbf{w}_{0k})\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}
be a sequence of smooth data converging to
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},h_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})
in
H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{s_{0}+1}\times H^{s_{0}}
, where
\mathbf{w}_{0k}
is defined by

\mathbf{w}_{0k}=\bar{\rho}^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho_{0k}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{0k}.

By Proposition
4.1
, for each data
(\mathbf{v}_{0k},\rho_{0k},h_{0k},\mathbf{w}_{0k})
, there exists a solution
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\rho_{k},h_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})
to System (
1.1
), and

(\mathbf{v}_{k},\rho_{k},h_{k},\mathbf{w}_{k})|_{t=0}=(\mathbf{v}_{0k},\rho_{0k},h_{0k},\mathbf{w}_{0k}),\quad\mathbf{w}_{k}=\bar{\rho}^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}_{k}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{k}.

We should note that the solutions of (
1.1
) also satisfy the symmetric hyperbolic system (
2.1
). Set
\mathbf{U}_{k}=(p_{k},\mathbf{v}_{k},h_{k})^{\mathrm{T}},k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
, where
p_{k}=\bar{\rho}^{\gamma}\mathrm{e}^{h_{k}+\gamma\rho_{k}}
. For
j\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
, we then have

\begin{split}&A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{k})\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{k}+\sum^{3}_{i=1}A^{i}(\mathbf{U}_{k})\partial_{i}\mathbf{U}_{k}=0,\\
&A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{l})\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{l}+\sum^{3}_{i=1}A^{i}(\mathbf{U}_{l})\partial_{i}\mathbf{U}_{l}=0.\end{split}

The standard energy estimates tell us that

\frac{d}{dt}\|\mathbf{U}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\leq C_{\mathbf{U}_{k},\mathbf{U}_{l}}\left(\|d\mathbf{U}_{k},d\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{U}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\mathbf{U}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\right),

where
C_{\mathbf{U}_{k},\mathbf{U}_{l}}
depends on the
L^{\infty}_{x}
norm of
\mathbf{U}_{k},\mathbf{U}_{l}
. By Strichartz estimates of
d\mathbf{v}_{k},d\rho_{k},dh_{k},k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
in Proposition
4.1
, we derive that

\begin{split}\|(\mathbf{U}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{l})(t,\cdot)\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{U}_{k}-\mathbf{U}_{l})(0,\cdot)\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\\
&\lesssim\|(\mathbf{v}_{0k}-\mathbf{v}_{0l},\rho_{0k}-\rho_{0l},h_{0k}-h_{0l})\|_{H^{s-1}}.\end{split}

As a result,
\{(p_{k},\mathbf{v}_{k},h_{k})\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s-1})
. Denote
(p,\mathbf{v},h)
being the limit. Then

\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}(p_{k},\mathbf{v}_{k},h_{k})=(p,\mathbf{v},h)\ \mathrm{in}\ C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s-1}).

Since
\rho_{k}=\frac{1}{\gamma}\ln(p_{k}\mathrm{e}^{-h_{k}}\bar{\rho}^{-\gamma})
, then

\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}\rho_{k}=\rho\ \mathrm{in}\ C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s-1}).

Consider

\mathbf{w}_{k}=\bar{\rho}^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-\rho_{k}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{k},\quad k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}.

Due to product estimates and elliptic estimates, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}_{k}-\mathbf{w}_{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}-2}}&\lesssim(\|\mathbf{v}_{k}-\mathbf{v}_{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\rho_{k}-\rho_{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}-1}})\\
&\lesssim(\|\mathbf{v}_{0k}-\mathbf{v}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|{\rho}_{0k}-{\rho}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}).\end{split}

(4.6)

Above, in the last line we use Lemma
2.8
. The estimate (
4.6
) implies that
\{\mathbf{w}_{k}\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s_{0}-2})
. We denote
\mathbf{w}
being the limit, that is

\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}\mathbf{w}_{k}=\mathbf{w}\ \mathrm{in}\ C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s_{0}-2}).

Since
(\mathbf{v}_{k},\rho_{k},h_{k})
is uniformly bounded in
C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s})
and
\mathbf{w}_{k}
is bounded in
C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s_{0}})
respectively. Thus,
(\mathbf{v},\rho,h)\in C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s}),\mathbf{w}\in C([-T,T];H_{x}^{s_{0}})
. Using Proposition
4.1
again, the sequence
\{(d\mathbf{v}_{k},d\rho_{k},dh_{k})\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}
is uniformly bounded in
L^{2}([-T,T];C_{x}^{\delta})
. Consequently, the sequence
\{(d\mathbf{v}_{k},d\rho_{k},dh_{k})\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}
converges to
(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)
in
L^{2}([-T,T];L_{x}^{\infty})
. That is,

\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}(d\mathbf{v}_{k},d\rho_{k},dh_{k})=(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh).

(4.7)

On the other hand, we also deduce that the sequence
\{(d\mathbf{v}_{k},d\rho_{k},dh_{k})\}_{k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}}
is bounded in
L^{2}([-T,T];\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2})
.
Combined with (
4.7
), this yields

(d\mathbf{v},d\rho,dh)\in L^{2}([-T,T];\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}).

(4.8)

By using (
4.3
), we have that

\mathbf{v},\rho,h\in L^{\infty}([-T,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}).

(4.9)

Set

\mathbf{v}=\mathbf{v}_{+}+\mathbf{v}_{-},\quad\mathbf{v}_{-}=(-\Delta)^{-1}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}).

We have

\begin{split}\|\partial\mathbf{v}_{+}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\leq\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}.\end{split}

(4.10)

Using elliptic estimates and Sobolev inequalities, we have

\begin{split}\|\partial\mathbf{v}_{-}\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}&\lesssim\|\partial(-\Delta)^{-1}(\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w})\|_{\dot{B}^{s_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}_{2,2}}\\
&\lesssim\|\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}\|_{\dot{H}^{s_{0}-\frac{3}{2}}}\\
&\lesssim(1+\|{\rho}_{0}\|_{H^{s}})\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{s_{0}}}.\end{split}

As a result, we have
\partial\mathbf{v}_{-}\in L^{2}([-T,T];\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2})
. By (
4.10
), we can obtain
\partial\mathbf{v}_{+}\in L^{2}([-T,T];\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2})
. This completes the proof of Theorem
1.4
.
