V^{A13}_{2}=&C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x},\mathbf{T}^{l+1}]\left\{\big(\partial^{k}(\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\big)\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\right\}\\
&\qquad\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\left\{\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\right\}dxd\tau\big|,\\
V^{A14}_{2}=&C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\left\{[\partial^{k},\mathbf{T}^{l+1}](\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\right\}\\
&\qquad\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\left\{\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\right\}dxd\tau\big|.\end{split}

Note
\mathbf{T}^{l+1}(\partial_{j}h^{l+1})=\partial\mathbf{v}^{l+1}\partial h^{l+1}
. By Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.18
and Hölder’s inequality, we derive that

\begin{split}V^{A12}_{2}+V^{A13}_{2}+V^{A14}_{2}\leq&C_{0}\int^{t}_{0}\|{\rho}^{l+1}-{\rho}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}\|h^{l+1}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}B(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(9.62)

For
V^{A121}_{2}
, we calculate it directly

\begin{split}V^{A11}_{2}=&C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\big\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial^{k}(\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\big)\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\\
&\qquad\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\big)\big\}dxd\tau\big|\\
&+C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial^{k}(\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\big)\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\right\}\\
&\qquad\cdot\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\right\}dxd\tau\big|\\
=&C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\big\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial^{k}(\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\big)\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\\
&\qquad\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\big)\big\}dxd\tau\big|\\
&+C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial^{k}(\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\big)\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\right\}\\
&\qquad\cdot\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\left\{\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\right\}dxd\tau\big|\\
&+C\big|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\{\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x}\big(\partial^{k}(\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l+1}-\partial^{j}{\rho}^{l})\big)\partial_{j}h^{l+1}\right\}\\
&\qquad\cdot[\Lambda^{s_{0}-2}_{x},\mathbf{T}^{l+1}]\left\{\partial_{k}(\Delta h^{l+1}-\Delta h^{l}-\partial^{i}h^{l+1}\partial_{i}\rho^{l+1}-\partial^{i}h^{l}\partial_{i}\rho^{l})\right\}dxd\tau\big|\end{split}

Using (
9.51
), Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.18
, and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\begin{split}V^{A11}_{2}\leq&C\|{\rho}_{0}^{l+1}-{\rho}_{0}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|h_{0}^{l+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H_{x}^{s_{0}+1}}B(0)\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\rho}^{l+1}-{\rho}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}\|_{\dot{B}_{\infty,2}^{s_{0}-2}}\|h^{l+1}\|_{H_{x}^{s_{0}}}B(\tau)d\tau.\end{split}

(9.63)

By using (
9.25
) and (
9.59
)-(
9.63
), for
t\in[0,\frac{1}{100(1+C)^{2}}T]
, we have

V_{2}\leq\frac{3}{2}(Cc_{l})^{2}.

(9.64)

By using (
9.25
) again and (
9.54
), (
9.55
),(
9.56
),(
9.57
) and (
9.64
), we can see that

B(t)\leq 3Cc_{l},\qquad t\in[0,\frac{1}{100(1+C)^{2}}T].

Hence, we can get

\|h^{l+1}-h^{l}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}+1}}\leq B(t)+\|\partial(h^{l+1}-h^{l})\partial\rho^{l+1}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}+\|\partial h^{l}\partial(\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l})\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s_{0}-1}}\leq 6Cc_{l}.

(9.65)

Step 4: Strichartz estimates of the difference
. Let us now use the wave equation (
2.3
) for
(\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l},h^{l},\mathbf{w}^{l})
. That is,

\begin{cases}&\square_{g^{l}}v_{+}^{il}=\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{T}^{l}v^{il}_{-}+Q^{il},\\
&\square_{g^{l}}(\rho^{l}+\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l})={D}^{l}+\frac{1}{\gamma}{E}^{l},\end{cases}

Above,
g^{l},\mathbf{v}^{l}_{+},Q^{il},E^{l}
and
{D}^{l}
have the same formulation with
g,Q^{i},E
and
{D}
in (
1.10
),(
1.12
),(
1.14
) if we replace
(\mathbf{v},\rho,h,\mathbf{w})
to
(\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l},h^{l},\mathbf{w}^{l})
. Thus, the quantities
\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l}
and
\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l}
satisfies

\begin{cases}&\square_{g^{l+1}}(\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l})=\mathbf{T}^{l+1}(\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}-\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-})-(g^{l+1}_{\alpha i}-g^{l}_{\alpha i})\partial^{i\alpha}\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l}+\mathbf{F}^{l},\\
&\square_{g^{l+1}}(\rho^{l+1}+\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l+1}-\rho^{l}-\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l})={G}^{l+1}-G^{l}-(g^{l+1}_{\alpha i}-g^{l}_{\alpha i})\partial^{i\alpha}(\rho^{l}+\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l}),\end{cases}

(9.66)

where
\mathbf{F}^{l}=\mathbf{Q}^{l+1}-\mathbf{Q}^{l}+(\mathbf{T}^{l+1}-\mathbf{T}^{l})\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l}
and
G^{l}={D}^{l}+\frac{1}{\gamma}{E}^{l}
. Let us now estimate the right hand side of (
9.66
) one by one. Calculate
\mathbf{T}^{l+1}-\mathbf{T}^{l}=(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l})\cdot\nabla
. By product estimates and (
1.8
), we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{F}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\leq&\|\mathbf{Q}^{l+1}-\mathbf{Q}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}+\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l})\cdot\nabla(\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|(d\rho^{l},d\mathbf{v}^{l},dh^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(d\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-d\mathbf{v}^{l},d\rho^{l+1}-d\rho^{l},dh^{l+1}-dh^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+\|(d\rho^{l+1},d\mathbf{v}^{l+1},dh^{l+1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(d\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-d\mathbf{v}^{l},d\rho^{l+1}-d\rho^{l},dh^{l+1}-dh^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+2^{l}\|\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}.\\
\leq&\|(\partial\rho^{l},\partial\mathbf{v}^{l},\partial h^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+\|\partial(\rho^{l+1},\mathbf{v}^{l+1},h^{l+1})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+2^{l}\|\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.67)

Using
\Delta\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}=\mathrm{e}^{\rho^{l}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{w}^{l}
, and recalling the proof of (
2.81
), we find out

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}-\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial\mathbf{v}^{l+1}\partial\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}\partial\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}^{l+1}\partial\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}-\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}^{l}\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
&+\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}\partial\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial^{2}\rho^{l+1}\partial h^{l+1}-\partial^{2}\rho^{l}\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
&+\|\partial h^{l+1}\partial\rho^{l+1}\partial h^{l+1}-\partial h^{l}\partial\rho^{l}\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}+\|\partial\rho^{l+1}\partial\rho^{l+1}\partial h^{l+1}-\partial\rho^{l}\partial\rho^{l}\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\\
&+\|\partial\rho^{l+1}\partial^{2}h^{l+1}-\partial\rho^{l}\partial^{2}h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}.\end{split}

(9.68)

By product estimates, (
9.68
) yields

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{T}^{l+1}\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}-\mathbf{T}^{l}\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial(\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\\
&+\|\partial^{2}(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}+\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\|\partial(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}-\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-})\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}\\
&+\|\partial(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial^{2}\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial^{2}(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}_{-}-\mathbf{v}^{l}_{-})\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\\
&+(\|\partial(h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial(\rho^{l+1}-\partial\rho^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}})\|\partial\rho^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\\
&+(\|\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial\rho^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}})\|\partial(\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\\
&+(\|\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial\rho^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}})\|\partial\rho^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial(h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\\
&+\|\partial(\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial^{2}h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}+\|\partial\rho^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-1}}\|\partial^{2}(h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{1}{2}+}}\\
&+\|\partial^{2}(\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\|\partial h^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}+\|\partial^{2}\rho^{l}\|_{H_{x}^{s-2}}\|\partial(h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+}}.\end{split}

(9.69)

By product estimates, Bernstein’s inequalities and (
1.8
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|(g^{l+1}_{\alpha i}-g^{l}_{\alpha i})\partial^{i\alpha}\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\lesssim&\|\partial d\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial d\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\cdot 2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial\mathbf{v}^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.70)

Similarly, we also deduce

\begin{split}&\|(g^{l+1}_{\alpha i}-g^{l}_{\alpha i})\partial^{i\alpha}(\rho^{l}+\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\|\partial(d\rho^{l},dh^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial(d\rho^{l},dh^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\partial\rho^{l},\partial h^{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\cdot 2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\\
&+2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial(\rho^{l},h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}},\end{split}

(9.71)

and

\begin{split}\|G^{l+1}-G^{l}\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|(\partial\rho^{l},\partial\mathbf{v}^{l},\partial h^{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{H^{s-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(9.72)

To the system (
9.66
), by using Proposition
7.1
, Lemma
2.14
and using (
9.25
) and (
9.67
)-(
9.72
), we can derive that

\begin{split}&2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l},\rho^{l+1}+\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l+1}-\rho^{l}-\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,\frac{1}{100(1+C)^{2}}T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&\quad+2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}_{+}^{l},\rho^{l+1}+\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l+1}-\rho^{l}-\frac{1}{\gamma}h^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,\frac{1}{100(1+C)^{2}}T]}\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\\
\leq&Cc^{2}_{l}\leq\frac{C}{2}c_{l},\end{split}

(9.73)

where we use the fact
c_{l}\rightarrow 0,l\rightarrow\infty
.
Moreover, by Sobolev imbeddings, we also have

\begin{split}&2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}_{x}}+2^{l}\|\partial(\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}_{-}^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{t}\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\\
\lesssim&\|\mathbf{w}^{l+1}-\mathbf{w}^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s_{0}-\frac{1}{2}}_{x}}+\|h^{l+1}-h^{l}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{s_{0}+\frac{1}{2}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{s}{2(s_{0}+1)}l}Cc_{l}\leq\frac{1}{4}Cc_{l}.\end{split}

(9.74)

Due to (
9.73
) and (
9.74
), we

\begin{split}&2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,\frac{1}{100(1+C)^{2}}T]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&\ +2^{l}\|(\mathbf{v}^{l+1}-\mathbf{v}^{l},\rho^{l+1}-\rho^{l},h^{l+1}-h^{l})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,\frac{1}{100(1+C)^{2}}T]}\dot{B}^{s_{0}-2}_{\infty,2}}\leq\frac{3C}{4}c_{l}.\end{split}

(9.75)

Therefore, by gathering (
9.32
), (
9.33
), (
9.48
), (
9.65
) and (
9.75
), we have proved (
9.10
)-(
9.11
).
∎

Remark 9.1
.

The estimate (
9.75
) is essential for us to prove the continuous dependence. If we consider the classical solutions, then we could use Sobolev imbeddings to handle (
9.66
). However, for rough solutions, it is necessary to use equation (
9.66
) and the Strichartz estimates (
1.36
) of the linear wave equation (
1.35
) to establish (
9.75
). We also mention that the continuous dependence of solutions of (
1.1
)-(
1.3
) has not been proved in
[
63
]
and
[
16
]
.

10.
Proof of Theorem
1.6

In this part, our goal is to prove Theorem
1.6
. We first reduce the proof to the case of smooth initial data with bounded frequency, that is, using Proposition
10.1
to prove Theorem
1.6
. Then we use Proposition
4.2
to obtain Proposition
10.2
. Finally, we use Proposition
10.2
and develop a “semiclassical analysis” approach to prove Proposition
10.1
.

10.1.
Proof of Theorem
1.6

To prove Theorem
1.6
, we will prove the well-posedness of solutions as a limit of a sequence. To find this sequence, let us introduce a sequence of initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j})
with frequency support such that

\mathbf{v}_{0j}=P_{\leq j}\mathbf{v}_{0},\quad\rho_{0j}=P_{\leq j}\rho_{0},

(10.1)

where
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0})
is stated as (
1.40
) in Theorem
1.6
, and
P_{\leq j}=\textstyle{\sum}_{k\leq j}P_{k}
. Following (
1.5
), so we define

\mathbf{w}_{0j}=\mathrm{e}^{-\rho_{0j}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{0j}.

(10.2)

Using (
1.40
), so we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}=&\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0j}}\mathrm{curl}\mathbf{v}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}\\
\leq&\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0j}}P_{\leq j}(\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0}}\mathbf{w}_{0})\|_{H^{2}}\\
\leq&C(\|\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{0}}\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{2}}+\|\rho_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\|\mathbf{w}_{0}\|_{H^{2}})\\
\leq&C(M_{*}+M^{2}_{*}).\end{split}

(10.3)

Adding (
10.1
) and (
10.3
), we can see

E(0):=\|\mathbf{v}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\rho_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}\leq C(M_{*}+M^{2}_{*}).

(10.4)

Using (
1.27
), (
10.1
) and (
10.2
), we get

|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}|\leq C_{0},\quad c_{s}|_{t=0}\geq c_{0}>0.

(10.5)

Before we give a proof of Theorem
1.6
, let us now introduce Proposition
10.1
.

Proposition 10.1
.

Let
s
and (
1.27
)-(
1.41
) be stated in Theorem
1.6
. Let
(\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j},\mathbf{w}_{0j})
be stated in (
10.1
) and (
10.2
). For each
j\geq 1
, consider Cauchy problem (
1.17
) with the initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j},\mathbf{w}_{0j})
. Then for all
j\geq 1
, there exists two positive constants
T^{*}>0
and
\bar{M}_{2}>0
(
T^{*}
and
\bar{M}_{2}
only dependS on
{M}_{*}
and
s
) such that (
1.17
) has a unique solution
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j})\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s})\cap C^{1}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s-1})
,
\mathbf{w}_{j}\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{2})\cap C^{1}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{1})
. To be precise,

\mathrm{(1)}
the solution
\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}
and
\mathbf{w}_{j}
satisfy the energy estimates

\|\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{2}}\leq\bar{M}_{2},

(10.6)

and

\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{j},\partial_{t}\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{1}}\leq\bar{M}_{2},

(10.7)

and

\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq 2+C_{0}.

(10.8)

\mathrm{(2)}
the solution
\mathbf{v}_{j}
and
\rho_{j}
satisfy the Strichartz estimate

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\leq\bar{M}_{2}.

(10.9)

\mathrm{(3)}
for
\frac{s}{2}\leq r\leq 3
, consider the following linear wave equation

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}f_{j}=0,\qquad[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
(f_{j},\partial_{t}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(f_{0j},f_{1j}),\end{cases}

(10.10)

where
(f_{0j},f_{1j})=(P_{\leq j}f_{0},P_{\leq j}f_{1})
and
(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H^{r-1}_{x}
. Then there is a unique solution
f_{j}
on
[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Moreover, for
a\leq r-\frac{s}{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}d{f}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq\bar{M}_{4}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq\bar{M}_{4}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}),\end{split}

(10.11)

where
\bar{M}_{4}
is a universal constant depends on
C_{0},c_{0},M_{*},s
.

Based on Proposition
10.1
, we are now ready to prove Theorem
1.6
.

Proof of Theorem
1.6
by Proposition
10.1
.

We will divide the proof into four steps, The following Step 1 and Step 2 is for Point 1 and Point 2 in Theorem
1.6
. Step 3 is for giving a proof to Point 3. At last, we prove Point 4 in Step 4.

Step 1: Obtaining a solution of Theorem
1.6
in a weaker spaces by taking a limit.

If we set
\mathbf{U}_{j}=(p(\rho_{j}),\mathbf{v}_{j})^{\mathrm{T}}
, by Lemma
2.1
and
2.4
, for
j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
we have

\begin{split}A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{t}\mathbf{U}_{j}+A^{i}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{i}\mathbf{U}_{j}=0,\\
\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j}+(\mathbf{v}_{j}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{w}_{j}=(\mathbf{w}_{j}\cdot\nabla)\mathbf{v}_{j}.\end{split}

Then for any
j,l\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
, we have

\begin{split}A^{0}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l})+A^{i}(\mathbf{U}_{j})\partial_{i}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l})=&-\{A^{\alpha}(\mathbf{U}_{j})-A^{\alpha}(\mathbf{U}_{l})\}\partial_{\alpha}\mathbf{U}_{l},\\
\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})+(\mathbf{v}_{j}\cdot\nabla)(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})=&-\{(\mathbf{v}_{j}-\mathbf{v}_{l})\cdot\nabla\}\mathbf{w}_{l}\\
&\ +\{(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})\cdot\nabla)\}\mathbf{v}_{j}\\
&\ +(\mathbf{w}_{l}\cdot\nabla)(\mathbf{v}_{j}-\mathbf{v}_{l}).\end{split}

(10.12)

Using (
10.6
)-(
10.9
), we can show that

\|\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}\leq C_{\bar{M}_{2}}(\|\mathbf{v}_{0j}-\mathbf{v}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\rho_{0j}-\rho_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

Here
C_{\bar{M}_{2}}
is a constant depending on
\bar{M}_{2}
. By Lemma
2.8
, so we get

\|\mathbf{v}_{j}-\mathbf{v}_{l},\rho_{j}-\rho_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{1}_{x}}\leq C_{\bar{M}_{2}}(\|\mathbf{v}_{0j}-\mathbf{v}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\rho_{0j}-\rho_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

Using (
1.17
) and (
2.4
) again, we also have

\|\partial_{t}(\mathbf{U}_{j}-\mathbf{U}_{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq C_{C_{*}}(\|\mathbf{v}_{0j}-\mathbf{v}_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\rho_{0j}-\rho_{0l}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}-\mathbf{w}_{0l}\|_{H^{2}}).

So the sequence
\{(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
H^{1}_{x}
, and
\{(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{j},\partial_{t}\rho_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
is a Cauchy sequence in
L^{2}_{x}
. So there is a limit
(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})
such that

\begin{split}(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})\rightarrow&(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})\quad\quad\ \ \quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{1}\times H_{x}^{1}\times H_{x}^{1},\\
(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{j},\partial_{t}\rho_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})\rightarrow&(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\rho,\partial_{t}\mathbf{w})\quad\text{in}\ \ L_{x}^{2}\times L_{x}^{2}\times L_{x}^{2}.\end{split}

(10.13)

By using (
10.6
) and (
10.7
), then a subsequence of
\{(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
and
\{(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{j},\partial_{t}\rho_{j},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j})\}_{j\in\mathbb{N}^{+}}
are weakly convergent. Therefore, if
m\rightarrow\infty
, then

\begin{split}(\mathbf{v}_{j_{m}},\rho_{j_{m}},\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}})\rightharpoonup&(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})\quad\qquad\ \ \text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s}\times H_{x}^{s}\times H_{x}^{2},\\
(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{j_{m}},\partial_{t}\rho_{j_{m}},\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}})\rightharpoonup&(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\rho,\partial_{t}\mathbf{w})\quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{s-1}\times H_{x}^{s-1}\times H_{x}^{1}.\end{split}

(10.14)

By (
10.13
), (
10.14
) and interpolation formula, we can obtain

(\mathbf{v}_{j_{m}},\rho_{j_{m}})\rightarrow(\mathbf{v},\rho)\quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{a}(0\leq a<s),\quad\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}}\rightarrow\mathbf{w}\quad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{b}(0\leq b<2).

(10.15)

By (
10.15
) and (
1.17
), (
1.18
) we also have

\begin{split}&(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}_{j_{m}},\partial_{t}\rho_{j_{m}})\rightarrow(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\rho)\ \qquad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{\alpha}(0\leq\alpha<s-1),\\
&\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}_{j_{m}}\rightarrow\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\text{in}\ \ H_{x}^{\beta}(0\leq\beta<1).\end{split}

(10.16)

The weak convergence (
10.14
) and the strong convergence (
10.15
)-(
10.16
) makes us easy to check that
(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})
is a strong solution of (
1.18
) with the initial data
(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})|_{t=0}=(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})
,
and for
1\leq a<s,1\leq b<2
,

\begin{split}&(\mathbf{v},\rho)\in C([0,T^{*}];H^{a}),\qquad\qquad\quad\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}];H^{b}),\\
&(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\rho)\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{a-1}),\qquad\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{b-1}),\\
&(\mathbf{v},\rho)\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s}),\qquad\qquad\mathbf{w}\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{2}),\\
&(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\rho)\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{s-1}),\ \quad\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\in L^{\infty}([0,T^{*}];H_{x}^{1}).\end{split}

(10.17)

Furthermore, by using (
10.6
), (
10.7
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\mathcal{E}(t)=\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{2}}\leq\bar{M}_{2},\end{split}

and

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}+\|d\rho\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\leq\bar{M}_{2},\\
&\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial_{t}\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s-1}}+\|\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{1}}\leq\bar{M}_{2}.\end{split}

It also remains for us that the convergence (

Step 2: Strong convergence of the limit in the highest derivatives.
Denote

\begin{split}e^{(a)}_{j}=&\|v^{a}_{0(j+1)}-v^{a}_{0j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},\quad a=1,2,3,\\
d^{(a)}_{j}=&\|w^{a}_{0(j+1)}-w^{a}_{0j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},\quad a=1,2,3,\\
e^{(4)}_{j}=&\|\rho_{0(j+1)}-\rho_{0j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.18)

Set

\begin{split}e_{j}=\textstyle{\sum}_{a=1}^{4}e^{(a)}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}_{a=1}^{3}d^{(a)}_{j}.\end{split}

(10.19)

Therefore, by using (
10.18
) and (
10.19
), we can deduce that

\begin{split}\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}e_{j}=0.\end{split}

(10.20)

Note (
10.1
) and (
10.2
). So we have these properties:

(i) uniform bounds

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}\lesssim M_{*}+M^{2}_{*},\quad\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{2}}\lesssim M_{*}+M^{2}_{*},\end{split}

(10.21)

(ii) high frequency bounds

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}\|_{H^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{3}}\lesssim 2^{j}(M_{*}+M^{2}_{*}),\end{split}

(10.22)

(iii) difference bounds

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0(j+1)}-\rho_{0j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sj}e_{j},\\
&\|\mathbf{w}_{0(j+1)}-\mathbf{w}_{0j}\|_{L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2j}e_{j},\end{split}

(10.23)

(iv) limit

\lim_{l\rightarrow\infty}(\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j},\mathbf{w}_{0j})=(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},\mathbf{w}_{0})\quad\mathrm{in}\quad H^{s}\times H^{s}\times H^{2}.

(10.24)

For solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
, we claim that

\bullet
uniform bounds

\|\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{2}}\lesssim\bar{M}_{2},

(10.25)

\bullet
higher-order norms

\|\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{s+1}}+\|\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H_{x}^{3}}\lesssim 2^{j}\bar{M}_{2},

(10.26)

\bullet
difference bounds

\|(\mathbf{v}_{j+1}-\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j+1}-\rho_{j})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sj}e_{j},

and

\|\mathbf{w}_{j+1}-\mathbf{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}\dot{H}_{x}^{2}}\lesssim e_{j},

(10.29)

\bullet
Strichartz estimates of the difference

2^{j}\|\mathbf{v}_{j+1}-\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j+1}-\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L_{x}^{\infty}}\lesssim e_{j}.

(10.30)

By using (

), (

) and (

), we get

\|\mathbf{v}_{j}-\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}.

Hence, combining with (

\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{v}_{j}-\mathbf{v}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}=0.

Similarly, using (

\begin{split}\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\rho_{j}-\rho\|_{H_{x}^{s}}\lesssim\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}=0,\\
\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}\|_{H_{x}^{2}}\lesssim\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\textstyle{\sum}_{l\geq j}e_{l}=0.\end{split}

(10.32)

Combining (
10.31
) and (

\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})=(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})
in

. In a similar way, we can show that

(\mathbf{v},\rho,\mathbf{w})

\begin{split}&(\mathbf{v},\rho)\in C([0,T_{*}];H^{s}),\qquad\qquad\quad\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T_{*}];H^{2}),\\
&(\partial_{t}\mathbf{v},\partial_{t}\rho)\in C([0,T_{*}];H_{x}^{s-1}),\qquad\partial_{t}\mathbf{w}\in C([0,T_{*}];H_{x}^{1}).\end{split}

\begin{split}\partial_{t}(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})+(\mathbf{v}_{j}\cdot\nabla)(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})=&-\{(\mathbf{v}_{j}-\mathbf{v}_{l})\cdot\nabla\}\mathbf{w}_{l}+\{(\mathbf{w}_{j}-\mathbf{w}_{l})\cdot\nabla)\}\mathbf{v}_{j}\\

Step 3: Obtaining a solution of linear wave by taking a limit.

Reapting the proof in Step 1 and Step 2, we shall also find

\begin{split}\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|f_{j}-f\|_{H^{r}_{x}}=0,\quad\lim_{j\rightarrow\infty}\|\partial_{t}f_{j}-\partial_{t}f\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}=0.\end{split}

(10.47)

So taking
j\rightarrow\infty
, we have

\begin{cases}&\square_{g}f=0,\qquad(t,x)\in(t_{0},T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
&(f,\partial_{t}f)|_{t=0}=(f_{0},f_{1})\in H^{r}\times H^{r-1}.\end{cases}

Using (
10.11
), it follows

\begin{split}&\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}d{f}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq\bar{M}_{4}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq\bar{M}_{4}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(10.48)

Step 4: Continuous dependence.

Based on (
10.48
), we are able to prove the continuous dependence of solutions on the initial data, which can follow Section
9
.

At this stage, we have finished the proof of Theorem
1.6
.

10.3.
Proof of Proposition
10.1
by using Proposition
10.2

In Proposition
10.2
, the corresponding statement considers the small, supported data. While, the initial data is large in Proposition
10.1
, and regularity of the vorticity in Proposition
10.1
is weaker than Proposition
10.2
. So we divide the proof into three subparts. Firstly, we can only get a solution of Proposition
10.1
on a short time interval, which is presented in subsection
10.3.1
. Then we are able to get some good Strichartz estimates for low-frequency and mid-frequency of solutions on these short time intervals by paying a loss of derivatives, which is proved in subsection
10.3.2
. After that, we can extend these solutions on a regular time-interval in subsection
10.3.3
. At the same time, the solutions of linear wave can also be extended to this regular time-interval, which is obtained in
10.3.4
.

10.3.1.
Energy estimates and Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval.

Firstly, by using a scaling method, we can reduce the initial data in Proposition

=&T^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}}\\

(10.54)

10.3.2.
A loss of Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval.

To see the energy estimates and Strichartz estimates of the sequence

Next, we will discuss it into two cases

, the high frequency and low frequency for

.

Case 1: High frequency(

Using (

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\leq C(1+E(0)),\quad a\in[0,s-2).\end{split}

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&2^{-ka}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-ja}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-ja}(1+E(0)).\end{split}

Taking

in (

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-7\delta_{1}j},,\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(10.84)

Case 2: Low frequency(

).
In this case, it’s a little different from

. Fortunately, there is some good estimates for difference terms

By Bernstein inequality, we get

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim&2^{-sm}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}.\end{split}

(10.86)

Similarly, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|{\rho}_{0(m+1)}-{\rho}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sm}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}},\\
&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2m}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}.\end{split}

(10.87)

We claim that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-\mathbf{v}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}E(0),

(10.88)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{w}_{m+1}-\mathbf{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\frac{1}{2}-\delta_{1})m}E(0).

(10.89)

Let
{\mathbf{U}}_{m}=(\mathbf{v}_{m},p(\rho_{m}))^{\mathrm{T}}
. Then
{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
satisfies

\begin{cases}&A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})\partial_{t}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})+A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})\partial_{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m=1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})=\Pi_{m},\\
&(\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-\widetilde{\mathbf{U}}_{m})|_{t=0}={\mathbf{U}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{0m},\end{cases}

(10.90)

where

\Pi_{m}=-[A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})-A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m})]\partial_{t}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}-[A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})-A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m})]\partial_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}.

(10.91)

By (
10.91
), we can see

|\Pi_{m}|\lesssim|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}|\cdot|d{\mathbf{U}}_{m}|.

(10.92)

Multiplying
{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
on (
10.90
) and integrating it on
\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\frac{d}{dt}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|(d{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1},d{\mathbf{U}}_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.

(10.93)

Integrating (
10.93
) on
[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
and using Gronwall’s inequality, (
10.76
), (
10.86
), and (
10.87
), it yields

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|{\mathbf{U}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}E(0).\end{split}

Then we get

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}E(0).\end{split}

So we have proved (
10.88
). To prove (
10.89
), let us consider the transport equation of
{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}
, that is,

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})+({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\cdot\nabla)({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})\\
=&-({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\cdot\nabla{\mathbf{w}}_{m}+({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})\cdot\nabla{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}+{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\cdot\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}).\end{split}

(10.94)

Multiplying with
{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}
on (
10.94
) and integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we derive

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\nabla{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C|\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\mathbf{w}}_{m}\cdot\nabla)({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\cdot({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})dxd\tau|.\end{split}

(10.95)

By calculating

\begin{split}&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\mathbf{w}}_{m}\cdot\nabla)({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\cdot({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})dxd\tau\\
=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\partial^{i}({v}^{j}_{m+1}-{v}^{j}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\{\partial^{i}({v}^{j}_{m+1}-{v}^{j}_{m})-\partial^{j}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\}\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\partial^{j}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\epsilon^{ij}_{\ \ k}(\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}_{m+1}}{w}^{k}_{m+1}-\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}_{m}}{w}^{k}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\partial^{j}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau,\end{split}

and

\begin{split}&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\partial^{j}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
=&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{j}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})(\mathrm{div}{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-\mathrm{div}{\mathbf{w}}_{m})dxd\tau\\
=&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{j}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}\cdot\nabla{\rho}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\cdot\nabla{\rho}_{m})dxd\tau,\end{split}

then we get

\begin{split}&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}({\mathbf{w}}_{m}\cdot\nabla)({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\cdot({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})dxd\tau\\
=&\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}\epsilon^{ij}_{\ \ k}(\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}_{m+1}}{w}^{k}_{m+1}-\mathrm{e}^{{\rho}_{m}}{w}^{k}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{j}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})\delta_{bj}({w}^{b}_{m+1}-{w}^{b}_{m})dxd\tau\\
&-\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m})({\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m})\cdot\nabla{\rho}_{m+1}dxd\tau.\\
&+\int^{t}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}{w}^{a}_{m}\delta_{ai}({v}^{i}_{m+1}-{v}^{i}_{m}){\mathbf{w}}_{m}\cdot\nabla({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})dxd\tau.\end{split}

(10.96)

Inserting (
10.96
) in (
10.95
), (
10.95
) yields

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|{\mathbf{w}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{w}}_{0m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1},\partial{\rho}_{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{2}}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{H^{1}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(10.97)

Seeing (
10.97
) and using (
10.76
), and (
10.88
), we can see that

\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\frac{1}{2}-\delta_{1})m}(1+E^{2}(0)).

So we have finished the proof of (
10.89
). On the other hand, using Strichartz estimates (
10.80
), then

\begin{split}&\|\partial({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial({\mathbf{v}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{+m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m},{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{2+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{3}{2}+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.98)

Noting
s=2+10\delta_{1}
and using (
10.88
)-(
10.89
), we can bound (
10.98
) by

\begin{split}&\|\partial({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial({\mathbf{v}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{+m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq 2^{-7\delta_{1}m}E(0).\end{split}

(10.99)

By (
10.89
) and Sobolev imbeddings, we shall get

\begin{split}\|\partial({\mathbf{v}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{-m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|\partial({\mathbf{v}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{-m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{2}(0))2^{-8\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(10.100)

If we add (
10.99
) and (
10.100
), then we have

\begin{split}&\|\partial({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{2}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(10.101)

By using (
1.17
) and (
10.101
), we shall obtain

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{t}({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\partial({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),\partial({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\cdot(1+\|\mathbf{v}_{m},\rho_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{s}_{x}})\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(10.102)

Due to (
10.101
) and (
10.102
), for
k<j
, it yields

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m},

(10.103)

and

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(10.104)

10.3.3.
Uniform energy and Strichartz estimates on a regular time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

Recall
T_{N_{0}}^{*}=[E(0)]^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}
, which is stated in (
10.55
). Recall (
10.84
) and (
10.103
). Therefore, when
k\geq j
and
j\geq N_{0}
, using (
10.84
) and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(10.105)

When
k<j
and
m\geq N_{0}
, using (
10.103
) and Hölder’s inequality, we also see

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(10.106)

On the other side, when
k\geq j
and
j<N_{0}
, using (
10.84
), we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(10.107)

When
k<j
and
m<N_{0}
, using (
10.103
) and Hölder’s inequality, we also see

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(10.108)

Due to a different time-interval for the sequency
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
, we will discuss the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
or
j\geq N_{0}+1
as follows.

Case 1:
j\leq N_{0}
.
In this case,
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
exists on
[0,T_{j}^{*}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{j}]
. So we don’t need to extend solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
. Using (
10.76
) and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+E(0)).

By (
10.58
), this yields

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2.

(10.109)

By the Newton-Leibniz formula and (
10.5
), it follows that

\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}|+\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0}.

(10.110)

Using Theorem
3.2
, we get

E(T^{*}_{N_{0}})\leq C(E(0)+E^{3}(0))\exp\{2(1+E^{3}(0))\textrm{e}^{2}\}.

(10.111)

Case 2:
j\geq N_{0}+1
.
In this case, we have to extend the time interval
I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. So we will use (
10.84
), (
10.103
), and Theorem
3.3
to calculate the energy at time
T_{j}^{*}
. Then we start at
T_{j}^{*}
and get a new time-interval.

To be simple, we set

I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]=[t_{0},t_{1}],\quad\quad|I_{1}|=[E(0)]^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

(10.112)

By frequency decomposition, we get

\begin{split}d\mathbf{v}_{j}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\\
=&P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k}.\end{split}

(10.113)

Similarly, we also have

\begin{split}d\rho_{j}=&P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\rho_{k}.\end{split}

(10.114)

When
k\geq j
, using (
10.105
) and (
10.107
), we have
25
25

25

In the case
j\geq N_{0}
we use (
10.105
). In the case
j<N_{0}
, we take
T^{*}_{j}=T^{*}_{N_{0}}
and use (
10.107
) to give a bound on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(10.115)

When
k<j
, using (
10.106
) and (
10.108
), we also see
26
26

26

In the case
m\geq N_{0}
we use (
10.106
). In the case
m<N_{0}
, we take
T^{*}_{m+1}=T^{*}_{N_{0}}
and use (
10.108
) to give a bound on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(10.116)

We will use and (
10.115
)-(
10.116
) to give a precise analysis of (
10.113
)-(
10.114
) and get some new time-intervals, and then we try to extend
\rho_{j}
from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Our strategy is as follows. In step 1, we extend it from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]
. In step 2, we use induction methods to conclude these estimates and also extend it to
[0,T_{N_{0}}^{*}]
.

Step 1: Extending
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]\ (j\geq N_{0}+1)
.
To start, referring (
10.55
), so we need to calculate
E(T^{*}_{j})
for obtaining a length of a new time-interval. Then we shall calculate
\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
. Using (
10.113
) and (
10.114
), we derive that

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

So we get

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(10.117)

By using (
10.117
) and (
10.5
), we get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq\|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}\|+\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C_{0}+2.\end{split}

(10.118)

By (
10.117
) and (
10.58
) and Theorem
3.3
, we have

\begin{split}E(T^{*}_{j})\leq C(E(0)+E^{3}(0))\exp\{2\textrm{e}^{2}(1+E^{3}(0))\}=C_{*}.\end{split}

(10.119)

Above,
C_{*}
is stated in (
10.59
). Starting at the time
T^{*}_{j}
, seeing (
10.55
) and (
10.119
), we shall get an extending time-interval with a length of
(C_{*})^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. But, if
T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}\geq T^{*}_{j-1}
, so we have finished this step. Else, we need to extend it again.

\mathit{Case1:T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}\geq T^{*}_{j-1}}
, then we get a new interval

I_{2}=[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}],\quad|I_{2}|=(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)E(0)^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

(10.120)

Moreover, referring (
10.115
) and (
10.116
), we can deduce

\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+C^{3}_{*})2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,

(10.121)

and
k<j
,

\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{j}-d{\rho}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+C^{3}_{*})2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)}.

(10.122)

Using (
10.58
) and
j\geq N_{0}+1
, (
10.121
) and (
10.122
) yields

\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,

(10.123)

and
k<j
,

\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{j}-d{\rho}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+E^{3}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}.

(10.124)

Therefore, we could get the following estimate

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.125)

Due to (
10.115
) and (
10.123
), it yields

\begin{split}\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}(2^{-7\delta_{1}j}+2^{-6\delta_{1}j})\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2.\end{split}

(10.126)

Due to (
10.116
) and (
10.124
), it yields

\begin{split}&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}(2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)}+2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)})\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2.\end{split}

(10.127)

Inserting (
10.126
)-(
10.127
) into (
10.125
), we derive that

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(10.128)

By (
10.128
), (
10.58
) and Theorem
3.3
, we also prove

\begin{split}&|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&E(T^{*}_{j-1})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

(10.129)

\mathit{Case2:T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}<T^{*}_{j-1}}
. In this situation, we will record

Referring (

\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+C^{3}_{*})2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,

(10.130)

\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{j}-d{\rho}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot C(1+C^{3}_{*})2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)},\quad k<j.

(10.131)

Similarly, we can also get

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\

Noting

if

, then it follows that

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\end{split}

(10.132)

Inserting (

), (

) and (

) and (

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{I_{1}\cup I_{2}}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(10.133)

By (

) and Theorem

, we also prove

Therefore, we can repeat the process with a length with

So we need a maximum of

+&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\end{split}

Due to (

), (

&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C_{*}E^{-1}(0)\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\

(10.136)

Therefore, by (

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq 2+C_{0},\\

At this moment, both in case 1 or case 2, seeing from (

&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(10.138)

As a result, we also have extended the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{m},\rho_{m},\mathbf{w}_{m})
(

to

. Moreover, referring (

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m},T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}+C(1+C^{2}_{*})2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C_{*}E^{-1}(0)\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}+C(1+E^{2}(0))2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1).\end{split}

(10.139)

For

, using (

), (

) yields

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}},\end{split}

(10.140)

Similarly, if
m\geq N_{0}+1
, using (
10.58
), then the following estimate holds for

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m}-d{\rho}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m}-d{\rho}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\

\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(m-1)}\times 2^{\delta_{1}}.\end{split}

(10.141)

Step 2: Extending time interval
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Based the above analysis in Step 1, we can give a induction by achieving the goal. We assume the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
can be extended from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]
through a maximam
X_{l}
times and

\begin{split}X_{l}=&\frac{T_{j-l}^{*}-T_{j}^{*}}{C^{-1}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}\\
=&\frac{E(0)^{-1}(2^{-\delta_{1}(j-l)}-2^{-\delta_{1}j})}{C^{-1}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}\\
=&\frac{C_{*}}{E(0)}(2^{\delta_{1}l}-1).\end{split}

(10.142)

Moreover, the following bounds

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l},\qquad k\geq j,\end{split}

(10.143)

and

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l},\quad k<j,\end{split}

(10.144)

and

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.

(10.145)

and

|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-l})\leq C_{*}.

(10.146)

In the following, we will check these estimates (
10.142
)-(
10.146
) hold when
l=1
, and then also holds when we replace

by

.

Using (

), (

), (

), and (

), then (

)-(

) hold by taking

. Let us now check it for

. Therefore,

should hold. Starting at the time

, seeing (

) and (

), we shall get an extending time-interval of
(\mathbf{v},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
with a length of

. So we can go to the case 2 in step 1, and the length every new time-interval is
C_{*}^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. So the times is

X=\frac{T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}-T^{*}_{j-l}}{C^{-1}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}=2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C_{*}E^{-1}(0).

(10.147)

Thus, we can deduce that

X_{l+1}=X_{l}+X=(2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}-1)C_{*}E^{-1}(0).

(10.148)

Moreover, for
k\geq j
, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.149)

Using (
10.115
) and (
10.146
)

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+C^{3}_{*})(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C_{*}E^{-1}(0),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(10.150)

Due to (
10.58
), we can see

\begin{split}(1+C^{3}_{*})C_{*}E^{-1}(0)(1+E^{3}(0))^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\leq 1.\end{split}

Hence, from (

) we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(10.151)

Using (
10.143
) and (
10.151
), so we get

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)},\qquad k\geq j.\end{split}

(10.152)

If
k<j
, then we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.153)

When we extend the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]
, then the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{m},\rho_{m},\mathbf{w}_{m})
is also extended from
[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]
. Seeing (
10.116
) and (
10.146
), we can obtain

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+C_{*}^{3})(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C_{*}E^{-1}(0).\end{split}

(10.154)

Hence, for
m-l\geq N_{0}+1
, using (
10.58
), it yields

(1+C_{*}^{3})C_{*}E^{-1}(0)(1+E^{3}(0))^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\leq 1.

So (
10.154
) becomes

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1).\end{split}

(10.155)

Inserting (
10.144
) and (
10.155
) to (
10.153
), we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)},\quad k<j,\end{split}

(10.156)

Let us now bound the following Strichartz estimate

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-(l+2)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}+\Theta_{3}+\Theta_{4}+\Theta_{5},\end{split}

(10.157)

where

\begin{split}\Theta_{1}=&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{2}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{3}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{4}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{5}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.158)

To get the estimate on time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]
for
\Theta_{1},\Theta_{2},\Theta_{3},\Theta_{4}
and
\Theta_{5}
, we should note that
there is no growth for
\Theta_{2}
and
\Theta_{4}
in this extending process. For example, considering
\Theta_{2}
, the existing time-interval of
P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m})
is actually
[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
if
m\geq j-(l+2)
. So we can use the initial bounds (
10.115
) and (
10.116
) to handle
\Theta_{2}
and
\Theta_{4}
. While, considering
\Theta_{1},\Theta_{3}
, and
\Theta_{5}
, we need to calculate the growth in the Strichartz estimate. Based on this idea, let us give a precise analysis on (
10.158
).

According to (
10.152
), we can estimate
\Theta_{1}
as

\begin{split}\Theta_{1}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}.\end{split}

(10.159)

Due to (
10.115
), we have

\begin{split}\Theta_{2}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(10.160)

For
1\leq k\leq j-1
and
m\leq j-1
, using (
10.156
), it follows

\begin{split}\Theta_{3}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}.\end{split}

(10.161)

Due to (
10.115
), we can see

\begin{split}\Theta_{4}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}.\end{split}

(10.162)

If
j-l\leq k\leq j-1
, then
k+l+1-j\leq l
. By using (
10.143
), we can estimate

\begin{split}\Theta_{5}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k-(k+l+1-j)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}2^{\delta_{1}(k+l+1-j)}.\end{split}

(10.163)

Inserting (
10.159
)-(
10.163
) to (
10.157
), it follows

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}k}2^{\delta_{1}(k+l+1-j)}\end{split}

(10.164)

In the case of
j-l\geq N_{0}+1
and
j\geq N_{0}+1
, (
10.164
) yields

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})^{-2}\big\{2^{-\delta_{1}j}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}\\
&\quad+2^{-5\delta_{1}[j-(l+1)]}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-l)}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1-j)}\big\}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})^{-2}\left\{2^{-6\delta_{1}N_{0}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-5\delta_{1}N_{0}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}N_{0}}\right\}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(10.165)

By using (
10.165
), (
10.5
), (
10.58
), and Theorem
3.3
, we have proved

\begin{split}|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-(l+1)})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

(10.166)

Gathering (
10.148
), (
10.152
), (
10.156
), (
10.165
) and (
10.166
), we know that (
10.142
)-(
10.146
) holding for
l+1
. So our induction hold (
10.142
)-(
10.146
) for
l=1
to
l=j-N_{0}
. Therefore, we can extend the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
when
j\geq N_{0}
. Let us denote

T^{*}=T^{*}_{N_{0}}=[E(0)]^{-1}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}.

(10.167)

Setting
l=j-N_{0}
in (
10.145
)-(
10.146
), we therefore get

\begin{split}&E(T^{*})\leq C_{*},\quad\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{1}{2}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2},\end{split}

(10.168)

where
N_{0}
and
C_{*}
depends on
C_{0},c_{0},s,M_{*}
. It is stated in (
10.58
) and (
10.59
). In this process, we can also conclude

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(10.169)

10.3.4.
Strichartz estimates of linear wave equation on time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

We still expect the behaviour of a linear wave equation endowed with
g_{j}=g(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j})
. So we claim a theorem as follows

Proposition 10.3
.

For
\frac{s}{2}\leq r\leq 3
, there is a solution
f_{j}
on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfying the following linear wave equation

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}f_{j}=0,\\
(f_{j},\partial_{t}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(f_{0j},f_{1j}),\end{cases}

(10.170)

where
(f_{0j},f_{1j})=(P_{\leq j}f_{0},P_{\leq j}f_{1})
and
(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H^{r-1}_{x}
. Moreover, for
a\leq r-\frac{s}{2}
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}d{f}_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{*}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{*}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(10.171)

Proof of Proposition
10.3
.

Our proof also relies a Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval. Then a loss of derivatives are then obtained by summing up the short time estimates with respect to these time intervals.

Using (
10.170
), (
10.79
) and (
10.80
), we have

\begin{split}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1+\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}}df_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|f_{0j}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\\
\leq&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\end{split}

(10.172)

where we use

a-1+\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}\leq r-\frac{s}{2}+\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}<r-1.

(10.173)

By Bernstein inequality, for
k\geq j
, we shall obtain

\begin{split}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}=&C2^{-\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}k}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1+\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}j}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1+\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}}df_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.174)

Combining (
10.172
) and (
10.174
), for
a\leq r-\frac{s}{2}
, we get

\begin{split}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(10.175)

On the other hand, for any integer
m\geq 1
, we also have

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m}}f_{m}=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{m}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
(f_{m},\partial_{t}f_{m})|_{t=0}=(f_{0m},f_{1m}),\end{cases}

and

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m+1}}f_{m+1}=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
(f_{m+1},\partial_{t}f_{m+1})|_{t=0}=(f_{0(m+1)},f_{1(m+1)}).\end{cases}

So the difference term
f_{m+1}-f_{m}
satisfies

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m+1}}(f_{m+1}-f_{m})=({g}^{\alpha i}_{m+1}-{g}^{\alpha i}_{m})\partial_{\alpha i}f_{m},\quad[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\\
(f_{m+1}-f_{m},\partial_{t}(f_{m+1}-f_{m}))|_{t=0}=(f_{0(m+1)}-f_{0m},f_{1(m+1)}-f_{1m}).\end{cases}

(10.176)

By Duhamel’s principle, (
10.80
) and (
10.173
), it yields

\begin{split}&\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|f_{0(m+1)}-f_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{r-\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}}}+C\|f_{1(m+1)}-f_{1m}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1-\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}}}\\
&\ +C\|({g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m})\cdot\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{9}{2}\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})+C\|{g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.177)

By using the energy estimates

\begin{split}\|\partial df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{m+1}]}H^{r}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|f_{0m}\|_{H^{r+1}_{x}}+\|f_{1m}\|_{H^{r}_{x}})\\
\leq&C2^{m}(\|f_{0m}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1m}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}).\end{split}

(10.178)

By using Strichartz estimates (
10.80
) and Lemma
2.14
again, we shall prove that

\begin{split}&2^{m}\|{g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&2^{m}(T^{*}_{m+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{m}(\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{1+\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{\mathbf{w}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{w}}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{1}}_{x}})\\
\leq&C2^{-7\delta_{1}m}(1+E^{3}(0)).\end{split}

(10.179)

Above, we use (
10.88
), (
10.89
), and (
10.55
).

Due to (
10.178
) and (
10.179
), for
k<j
, and
k\leq m
, (
10.177
) yields

\begin{split}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}m}\|(f_{0},f_{1})\|_{H^{r}_{x}\times H^{r-1}_{x}}(1+E^{3}(0)).\end{split}

(10.180)

In the following part, let us consider the energy estimates. Taking the operator
\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}
on (
10.170
), then we get

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}f_{j}=[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]f_{j},\\
(\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}f_{j},\partial_{t}\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}f_{0j},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}f_{1j}).\end{cases}

(10.181)

To estimate
[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}
, we will divide it into two cases
\frac{s}{2}<r\leq s
and
s<r\leq 3
.

Case 1:
\frac{s}{2}<r\leq s
.
For
\frac{s}{2}<r\leq s
, note that

\begin{split}[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}=&[{g}^{\alpha i}_{j}-\mathbf{m}^{\alpha i},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}\partial_{i}]\partial_{\alpha}f_{j}+\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}(\partial_{i}g_{j}\partial_{\alpha}f_{j})\\
=&[{g}_{j}-\mathbf{m},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}\nabla]df_{j}+\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}(\nabla g_{j}df).\end{split}

(10.182)

By (
10.182
) and Kato-Ponce estimates, we have
27
27

27

If
r=s
, then
L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}=L^{\infty}_{x}
.

\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{3}{\frac{3}{2}-s+r}}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}})

(10.183)

By Sobolev’s inequality, it follows

\|\left<\partial\right>^{r}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{3}{\frac{3}{2}-s+r}}_{x}}\leq C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(10.184)

By Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and Young’s inequality, we can get

\begin{split}\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{s-2}{3-s}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{5-2s}{3-s}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\end{split}

(10.185)

Case 2:
s<r\leq 3
.
For
s<r\leq 3
, using Kato-Ponce estimates, we have

\begin{split}&\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
=&\|{g}^{\alpha i}_{j}-\mathbf{m}^{\alpha i},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]\partial_{\alpha i}f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{3}{\frac{1}{2}-s+r}}_{x}}\|\nabla df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{1+s-r}}_{x}}).\end{split}

(10.186)

By Sobolev’s inequality, it follows

\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{3}{\frac{1}{2}-s+r}}_{x}}\leq C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(10.187)

By Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and Young’s inequality, we can get

\begin{split}\|\nabla df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{s-2}{3-s}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-1-\frac{s}{2}}df_{j}\|^{\frac{5-2s}{3-s}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\end{split}

(10.188)

For
\frac{s}{2}<r\leq 3
, gathering (
10.183
)-(
10.185
) with (
10.186
)- (
10.188
), so we have

\begin{split}\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\partial\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.189)

By (
10.181
) and (
10.189
), we get

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(10.190)

Using Gronwall’s inequality, we get

\begin{split}\|df_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C(\|df_{j}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\\
&\qquad\cdot\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\}.\end{split}

(10.191)

Note

\begin{split}\|f_{j}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|f_{j}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial_{t}f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(10.192)

By (
10.191
), (
10.192
) and (
10.168
), if
t\in[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
, then it follows

\begin{split}\|f_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|df_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\textrm{e}^{C_{*}}(\|f_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C_{*})\\
&\quad\cdot\exp\{\int^{t}_{0}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\cdot\textrm{e}^{\int^{t}_{0}\|\left<\partial\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau}\}.\end{split}

(10.193)

Based on (
10.175
), (
10.180
), and (
10.193
), following the extending method in subsecion
10.3.2
, for
a\leq r-\frac{s}{2}
we shall obtain

\begin{split}\|\left<\partial\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\times\{2^{\delta_{1}(j-N_{0})}\}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}N_{0}}2^{-\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}k}2^{-2\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(10.194)

and
